```
   166→def parse_model_input(raw: str, current_provider: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
   167→    """Parse ``/model`` input into ``(provider, model)``.
   168→
   169→    Supports ``provider:model`` syntax to switch providers at runtime::
   170→
   171→        openrouter:anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5  →  ("openrouter", "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5")
   172→        nous:hermes-3                           →  ("nous", "hermes-3")
   173→        anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5             →  (current_provider, "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5")
   174→        gpt-5.4                                 →  (current_provider, "gpt-5.4")
   175→
   176→    The colon is only treated as a provider delimiter if the left side is a
   177→    recognized provider name or alias.  This avoids misinterpreting model names
   178→    that happen to contain colons (e.g. ``anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet:beta``).
   179→
   180→    Returns ``(provider, model)`` where *provider* is either the explicit
   181→    provider from the input or *current_provider* if none was specified.
   182→    """
   183→    stripped = raw.strip()
   184→    colon = stripped.find(":")
   185→    if colon > 0:
   186→        provider_part = stripped[:colon].strip().lower()
   187→        model_part = stripped[colon + 1:].strip()
   188→        if provider_part and model_part and provider_part in _KNOWN_PROVIDER_NAMES:
   189→            return (normalize_provider(provider_part), model_part)
   190→    return (current_provider, stripped)
   191→
   192→
   193→def curated_models_for_provider(provider: Optional[str]) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   194→    """Return ``(model_id, description)`` tuples for a provider's model list.
   195→
   196→    Tries to fetch the live model list from the provider's API first,
   197→    falling back to the static ``_PROVIDER_MODELS`` catalog if the API
   198→    is unreachable.
   199→    """
   200→    normalized = normalize_provider(provider)
   201→    if normalized == "openrouter":
   202→        return list(OPENROUTER_MODELS)
   203→
   204→    # Try live API first (Codex, Nous, etc. all support /models)
   205→    live = provider_model_ids(normalized)
   206→    if live:
   207→        return [(m, "") for m in live]
   208→
   209→    # Fallback to static catalog
   210→    models = _PROVIDER_MODELS.get(normalized, [])
   211→    return [(m, "") for m in models]
   212→
   213→
   214→def normalize_provider(provider: Optional[str]) -> str:
   215→    """Normalize provider aliases to Hermes' canonical provider ids.
   216→
   217→    Note: ``"auto"`` passes through unchanged — use
   218→    ``hermes_cli.auth.resolve_provider()`` to resolve it to a concrete
   219→    provider based on credentials and environment.
   220→    """
   221→    normalized = (provider or "openrouter").strip().lower()
   222→    return _PROVIDER_ALIASES.get(normalized, normalized)
   223→
   224→
   225→def provider_model_ids(provider: Optional[str]) -> list[str]:
   226→    """Return the best known model catalog for a provider.
   227→
   228→    Tries live API endpoints for providers that support them (Codex, Nous),
   229→    falling back to static lists.
   230→    """
   231→    normalized = normalize_provider(provider)
   232→    if normalized == "openrouter":
   233→        return model_ids()
   234→    if normalized == "openai-codex":
   235→        from hermes_cli.codex_models import get_codex_model_ids
   236→
   237→        return get_codex_model_ids()
   238→    if normalized == "nous":
   239→        # Try live Nous Portal /models endpoint
   240→        try:
   241→            from hermes_cli.auth import fetch_nous_models, resolve_nous_runtime_credentials
   242→            creds = resolve_nous_runtime_credentials()
   243→            if creds:
   244→                live = fetch_nous_models(creds.get("api_key", ""), creds.get("base_url", ""))
   245→                if live:
   246→                    return live
   247→        except Exception:
   248→            pass
   249→    if normalized == "anthropic":
   250→        live = _fetch_anthropic_models()
   251→        if live:
   252→            return live
   253→    return list(_PROVIDER_MODELS.get(normalized, []))
   254→
   255→
   256→def _fetch_anthropic_models(timeout: float = 5.0) -> Optional[list[str]]:
   257→    """Fetch available models from the Anthropic /v1/models endpoint.
   258→
   259→    Uses resolve_anthropic_token() to find credentials (env vars or
   260→    Claude Code auto-discovery).  Returns sorted model IDs or None.
   261→    """
   262→    try:
   263→        from agent.anthropic_adapter import resolve_anthropic_token, _is_oauth_token
   264→    except ImportError:
   265→        return None
   266→
   267→    [REDACTED]()
   268→    if not token:
   269→        return None
   270→
   271→    headers: dict[str, str] = {"anthropic-version": "2023-06-01"}
   272→    if _is_oauth_token(token):
   273→        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {token}"
   274→        from agent.anthropic_adapter import _COMMON_BETAS, _OAUTH_ONLY_BETAS
   275→        headers["anthropic-beta"] = ",".join(_COMMON_BETAS + _OAUTH_ONLY_BETAS)
   276→    else:
   277→        headers["x-api-key"] = token
   278→
   279→    req = urllib.request.Request(
   280→        "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/models",
   281→        headers=headers,
   282→    )
   283→    try:
   284→        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
   285→            data = json.loads(resp.read().decode())
   286→            models = [m["id"] for m in data.get("data", []) if m.get("id")]
   287→            # Sort: latest/largest first (opus > sonnet > haiku, higher version first)
   288→            return sorted(models, key=lambda m: (
   289→                "opus" not in m,      # opus first
   290→                "sonnet" not in m,    # then sonnet
   291→                "haiku" not in m,     # then haiku
   292→                m,                    # alphabetical within tier
   293→            ))
   294→    except Exception as e:
   295→        import logging
   296→        logging.getLogger(__name__).debug("Failed to fetch Anthropic models: %s", e)
   297→        return None
   298→
   299→
   300→def fetch_api_models(
   301→    api_key: Optional[str],
   302→    base_url: Optional[str],
   303→    timeout: float = 5.0,
   304→) -> Optional[list[str]]:
   305→    """Fetch the list of available model IDs from the provider's ``/models`` endpoint.
   306→
   307→    Returns a list of model ID strings, or ``None`` if the endpoint could not
   308→    be reached (network error, timeout, auth failure, etc.).
   309→    """
   310→    if not base_url:
   311→        return None
   312→
   313→    url = base_url.rstrip("/") + "/models"
   314→    headers: dict[str, str] = {}
   315→    if api_key:
   316→        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {api_key}"
   317→
   318→    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers)
   319→    try:
   320→        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
   321→            data = json.loads(resp.read().decode())
   322→            # Standard OpenAI format: {"data": [{"id": "model-name", ...}, ...]}
   323→            return [m.get("id", "") for m in data.get("data", [])]
   324→    except Exception:
   325→        return None
   326→
   327→
   328→def validate_requested_model(
   329→    model_name: str,
   330→    provider: Optional[str],
   331→    *,
   332→    api_key: Optional[str] = None,
   333→    base_url: Optional[str] = None,
   334→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   335→    """
   336→    Validate a ``/model`` value for the active provider.
   337→
   338→    Performs format checks first, then probes the live API to confirm
   339→    the model actually exists.
   340→
   341→    Returns a dict with:
   342→      - accepted: whether the CLI should switch to the requested model now
   343→      - persist: whether it is safe to save to config
   344→      - recognized: whether it matched a known provider catalog
   345→      - message: optional warning / guidance for the user
   346→    """
   347→    requested = (model_name or "").strip()
   348→    normalized = normalize_provider(provider)
   349→    if normalized == "openrouter" and base_url and "openrouter.ai" not in base_url:
   350→        normalized = "custom"
   351→
   352→    if not requested:
   353→        return {
   354→            "accepted": False,
   355→            "persist": False,
   356→            "recognized": False,
   357→            "message": "Model name cannot be empty.",
   358→        }
   359→
   360→    if any(ch.isspace() for ch in requested):
   361→        return {
   362→            "accepted": False,
   363→            "persist": False,
   364→            "recognized": False,
   365→            "message": "Model names cannot contain spaces.",
   366→        }
   367→
   368→    # Custom endpoints can serve any model — skip validation
   369→    if normalized == "custom":
   370→        return {
   371→            "accepted": True,
   372→            "persist": True,
   373→            "recognized": False,
   374→            "message": None,
   375→        }
   376→
   377→    # Probe the live API to check if the model actually exists
   378→    api_models = fetch_api_models(api_key, base_url)
   379→
   380→    if api_models is not None:
   381→        if requested in set(api_models):
   382→            # API confirmed the model exists
   383→            return {
   384→                "accepted": True,
   385→                "persist": True,
   386→                "recognized": True,
   387→                "message": None,
   388→            }
   389→        else:
   390→            # API responded but model is not listed.  Accept anyway —
   391→            # the user may have access to models not shown in the public
   392→            # listing (e.g. Z.AI Pro/Max plans can use glm-5 on coding
   393→            # endpoints even though it's not in /models).  Warn but allow.
   394→            suggestions = get_close_matches(requested, api_models, n=3, cutoff=0.5)
   395→            suggestion_text = ""
   396→            if suggestions:
   397→                suggestion_text = "\n  Similar models: " + ", ".join(f"`{s}`" for s in suggestions)
   398→
   399→            return {
   400→                "accepted": True,
   401→                "persist": True,
   402→                "recognized": False,
   403→                "message": (
   404→                    f"Note: `{requested}` was not found in this provider's model listing. "
   405→                    f"It may still work if your plan supports it."
   406→                    f"{suggestion_text}"
   407→                ),
   408→            }
   409→
   410→    # api_models is None — couldn't reach API.  Accept and persist,
   411→    # but warn so typos don't silently break things.
   412→    provider_label = _PROVIDER_LABELS.get(normalized, normalized)
   413→    return {
   414→        "accepted": True,
   415→        "persist": True,
   416→        "recognized": False,
   417→        "message": (
   418→            f"Could not reach the {provider_label} API to validate `{requested}`. "
   419→            f"If the service isn't down, this model may not be valid."
   420→        ),
   421→    }
   422→
```

```
1→"""
     2→switch_model tool — lets the agent discover available models/providers
     3→and switch to a different one mid-conversation.
     4→
     5→Two actions:
     6→  - list: show current model, original model, and available providers+models
     7→  - switch: change to a different provider/model at runtime
     8→"""
     9→
    10→import json
    11→import logging
    12→from typing import Optional
    13→
    14→from tools.registry import registry
    15→
    16→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    17→
    18→
    19→def switch_model_list(
    20→    current_provider: str,
    21→    current_model: str,
    22→    original: dict,
    23→) -> str:
    24→    """Return JSON describing current state and available providers/models."""
    25→    from hermes_cli.models import list_available_providers, curated_models_for_provider
    26→
    27→    available = []
    28→    for prov in list_available_providers():
    29→        models = curated_models_for_provider(prov["id"])
    30→        available.append({
    31→            "provider": prov["id"],
    32→            "label": prov["label"],
    33→            "authenticated": prov["authenticated"],
    34→            "models": [m_id for m_id, _ in models],
    35→        })
    36→
    37→    return json.dumps({
    38→        "current": {"provider": current_provider, "model": current_model},
    39→        "original": original,
    40→        "available_providers": available,
    41→    }, ensure_ascii=False)
    42→
    43→
    44→def switch_model_switch(
    45→    provider: str,
    46→    model: str,
    47→    reason: str,
    48→    agent,  # AIAgent — imported at call site to avoid circular import
    49→) -> str:
    50→    """Ask the agent to switch its model/provider in-place.
    51→
    52→    Delegates to agent._switch_model() which handles client construction.
    53→    Returns JSON the agent sees as its tool result.
    54→    """
    55→    if not provider or not model:
    56→        return json.dumps({
    57→            "success": False,
    58→            "error": "Both 'provider' and 'model' are required for switch action.",
    59→            "current": {"provider": agent.provider, "model": agent.model},
    60→        }, ensure_ascii=False)
    61→
    62→    if reason:
    63→        logger.info("switch_model reason: %s", reason)
    64→
    65→    result = agent._switch_model(provider, model)
    66→    return json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False)
    67→
    68→
    69→# ── Schema ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    70→
    71→SWITCH_MODEL_SCHEMA = {
    72→    "name": "switch_model",
    73→    "description": (
    74→        "Discover available LLM providers/models and switch to a different one "
    75→        "mid-conversation. Use action='list' to see what's available, then "
    76→        "action='switch' to change. Consider switching to a faster/cheaper model "
    77→        "for simple tasks, or a more capable model for complex reasoning."
    78→    ),
    79→    "parameters": {
    80→        "type": "object",
    81→        "properties": {
    82→            "action": {
    83→                "type": "string",
    84→                "enum": ["list", "switch"],
    85→                "description": "Action to perform: 'list' shows available models, 'switch' changes the active model.",
    86→            },
    87→            "provider": {
    88→                "type": "string",
    89→                "description": "Provider to switch to (required for 'switch'). E.g. 'openrouter', 'nous', 'anthropic'.",
    90→            },
    91→            "model": {
    92→                "type": "string",
    93→                "description": "Model ID to switch to (required for 'switch'). E.g. 'anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6'.",
    94→            },
    95→            "reason": {
    96→                "type": "string",
    97→                "description": "Optional: why you're switching (logged for observability).",
    98→            },
    99→        },
   100→        "required": ["action"],
   101→    },
   102→}
   103→
   104→
   105→def _check_switch_model() -> bool:
   106→    """Always available when loaded."""
   107→    return True
   108→
   109→
   110→# ── Registration ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   111→
   112→registry.register(
   113→    name="switch_model",
   114→    toolset="switch_model",
   115→    schema=SWITCH_MODEL_SCHEMA,
   116→    # Handler is a stub — actual dispatch happens in run_agent._invoke_tool()
   117→    # because this tool needs agent-level state access.
   118→    handler=lambda args, **kw: json.dumps({
   119→        "error": "switch_model must be handled by the agent loop"
   120→    }),
   121→    check_fn=_check_switch_model,
   122→)
   123→
```

```
1→"""Slash command definitions and autocomplete for the Hermes CLI.
     2→
     3→Contains the shared built-in ``COMMANDS`` dict and ``SlashCommandCompleter``.
     4→The completer can optionally include dynamic skill slash commands supplied by the
     5→interactive CLI.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
    11→from typing import Any
    12→
    13→from prompt_toolkit.completion import Completer, Completion
    14→
    15→
    16→# Commands organized by category for better help display
    17→COMMANDS_BY_CATEGORY = {
    18→    "Session": {
    19→        "/new": "Start a new conversation (reset history)",
    20→        "/reset": "Reset conversation only (keep screen)",
    21→        "/clear": "Clear screen and reset conversation (fresh start)",
    22→        "/history": "Show conversation history",
    23→        "/save": "Save the current conversation",
    24→        "/retry": "Retry the last message (resend to agent)",
    25→        "/undo": "Remove the last user/assistant exchange",
    26→        "/title": "Set a title for the current session (usage: /title My Session Name)",
    27→        "/compress": "Manually compress conversation context (flush memories + summarize)",
    28→        "/rollback": "List or restore filesystem checkpoints (usage: /rollback [number])",
    29→        "/background": "Run a prompt in the background (usage: /background <prompt>)",
    30→    },
    31→    "Configuration": {
    32→        "/config": "Show current configuration",
    33→        "/model": "Show or change the current model",
    34→        "/provider": "Show available providers and current provider",
    35→        "/prompt": "View/set custom system prompt",
    36→        "/personality": "Set a predefined personality",
    37→        "/verbose": "Cycle tool progress display: off → new → all → verbose",
    38→        "/reasoning": "Manage reasoning effort and display (usage: /reasoning [level|show|hide])",
    39→        "/skin": "Show or change the display skin/theme",
    40→    },
    41→    "Tools & Skills": {
    42→        "/tools": "List available tools",
    43→        "/toolsets": "List available toolsets",
    44→        "/skills": "Search, install, inspect, or manage skills from online registries",
    45→        "/cron": "Manage scheduled tasks (list, add, remove)",
    46→        "/reload-mcp": "Reload MCP servers from config.yaml",
    47→    },
    48→    "Info": {
    49→        "/help": "Show this help message",
    50→        "/usage": "Show token usage for the current session",
    51→        "/insights": "Show usage insights and analytics (last 30 days)",
    52→        "/platforms": "Show gateway/messaging platform status",
    53→        "/paste": "Check clipboard for an image and attach it",
    54→    },
    55→    "Exit": {
    56→        "/quit": "Exit the CLI (also: /exit, /q)",
    57→    },
    58→}
    59→
    60→# Flat dict for backwards compatibility and autocomplete
    61→COMMANDS = {}
    62→for category_commands in COMMANDS_BY_CATEGORY.values():
    63→    COMMANDS.update(category_commands)
    64→
    65→
    66→class SlashCommandCompleter(Completer):
    67→    """Autocomplete for built-in slash commands and optional skill commands."""
    68→
    69→    def __init__(
    70→        self,
    71→        skill_commands_provider: Callable[[], Mapping[str, dict[str, Any]]] | None = None,
    72→    ) -> None:
    73→        self._skill_commands_provider = skill_commands_provider
    74→
    75→    def _iter_skill_commands(self) -> Mapping[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    76→        if self._skill_commands_provider is None:
    77→            return {}
    78→        try:
    79→            return self._skill_commands_provider() or {}
    80→        except Exception:
    81→            return {}
    82→
    83→    @staticmethod
    84→    def _completion_text(cmd_name: str, word: str) -> str:
    85→        """Return replacement text for a completion.
    86→
    87→        When the user has already typed the full command exactly (``/help``),
    88→        returning ``help`` would be a no-op and prompt_toolkit suppresses the
    89→        menu. Appending a trailing space keeps the dropdown visible and makes
    90→        backspacing retrigger it naturally.
    91→        """
    92→        return f"{cmd_name} " if cmd_name == word else cmd_name
    93→
    94→    def get_completions(self, document, complete_event):
    95→        text = document.text_before_cursor
    96→        if not text.startswith("/"):
    97→            return
    98→
    99→        word = text[1:]
   100→
   101→        for cmd, desc in COMMANDS.items():
   102→            cmd_name = cmd[1:]
   103→            if cmd_name.startswith(word):
   104→                yield Completion(
   105→                    self._completion_text(cmd_name, word),
   106→                    start_position=-len(word),
   107→                    display=cmd,
   108→                    display_meta=desc,
   109→                )
   110→
   111→        for cmd, info in self._iter_skill_commands().items():
   112→            cmd_name = cmd[1:]
   113→            if cmd_name.startswith(word):
   114→                description = str(info.get("description", "Skill command"))
   115→                short_desc = description[:50] + ("..." if len(description) > 50 else "")
   116→                yield Completion(
   117→                    self._completion_text(cmd_name, word),
   118→                    start_position=-len(word),
   119→                    display=cmd,
   120→                    display_meta=f"⚡ {short_desc}",
   121→                )
   122→
```

```
1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""
     3→AI Agent Runner with Tool Calling

   112→
   113→class _SafeWriter:
   114→    """Transparent stdout wrapper that catches OSError from broken pipes.
   115→
   116→    When hermes-agent runs as a systemd service, Docker container, or headless
   117→    daemon, the stdout pipe can become unavailable (idle timeout, buffer
   118→    exhaustion, socket reset). Any print() call then raises
   119→    ``OSError: [Errno 5] Input/output error``, which can crash
   120→    run_conversation() — especially via double-fault when the except handler
   121→    also tries to print.
   122→
   123→    This wrapper delegates all writes to the underlying stream and silently
   124→    catches OSError.  It is installed once at the start of run_conversation()
   125→    and is transparent when stdout is healthy (zero overhead on the happy path).
   126→    """
   127→
   128→    __slots__ = ("_inner",)
   129→
   130→    def __init__(self, inner):
   131→        object.__setattr__(self, "_inner", inner)
   132→
   133→    def write(self, data):
   134→        try:
   135→            return self._inner.write(data)
   136→        except OSError:
   137→            return len(data) if isinstance(data, str) else 0
   138→
   139→    def flush(self):
   140→        try:
   141→            self._inner.flush()
   142→        except OSError:
   143→            pass
   144→
   145→    def fileno(self):
   146→        return self._inner.fileno()
   147→
   148→    def isatty(self):
   149→        try:
   150→            return self._inner.isatty()
   151→        except OSError:
   152→            return False
   153→
   154→    def __getattr__(self, name):
   155→        return getattr(self._inner, name)
   156→
   157→
   158→class IterationBudget:
   159→    """Thread-safe shared iteration counter for parent and child agents.
   160→
   161→    Tracks total LLM-call iterations consumed across a parent agent and all
   162→    its subagents.  A single ``IterationBudget`` is created by the parent
   163→    and passed to every child so they share the same cap.
   164→
   165→    ``execute_code`` (programmatic tool calling) iterations are refunded via
   166→    :meth:`refund` so they don't eat into the budget.
   167→    """
   168→
   169→    def __init__(self, max_total: int):
   170→        self.max_total = max_total
   171→        self._used = 0
   172→        self._lock = threading.Lock()
   173→
   174→    def consume(self) -> bool:
   175→        """Try to consume one iteration.  Returns True if allowed."""
   176→        with self._lock:
   177→            if self._used >= self.max_total:
   178→                return False
   179→            self._used += 1
   180→            return True
   181→
   182→    def refund(self) -> None:
   183→        """Give back one iteration (e.g. for execute_code turns)."""
   184→        with self._lock:
   185→            if self._used > 0:
   186→                self._used -= 1
   187→
   188→    @property
   189→    def used(self) -> int:
   190→        return self._used
   191→
   192→    @property
   193→    def remaining(self) -> int:
   194→        with self._lock:
   195→            return max(0, self.max_total - self._used)
   196→
   197→
   198→# Tools that must never run concurrently (interactive / user-facing).
   199→# When any of these appear in a batch, we fall back to sequential execution.
   200→_NEVER_PARALLEL_TOOLS = frozenset({"clarify"})
   201→
   202→# Maximum number of concurrent worker threads for parallel tool execution.
   203→_MAX_TOOL_WORKERS = 8
   204→
   205→
   206→class AIAgent:
   207→    """
   208→    AI Agent with tool calling capabilities.
   209→    
   210→    This class manages the conversation flow, tool execution, and response handling
   211→    for AI models that support function calling.
   212→    """
   213→    
   214→    def __init__(
   215→        self,
   216→        base_url: str = None,
   217→        api_key: str = None,
   218→        provider: str = None,
   219→        api_mode: str = None,
   220→        model: str = "anthropic/claude-opus-4.6",  # OpenRouter format
   221→        max_iterations: int = 90,  # Default tool-calling iterations (shared with subagents)
   222→        tool_delay: float = 1.0,
   223→        enabled_toolsets: List[str] = None,
   224→        disabled_toolsets: List[str] = None,
   225→        save_trajectories: bool = False,
   226→        verbose_logging: bool = False,
   227→        quiet_mode: bool = False,
   228→        ephemeral_system_prompt: str = None,
   229→        log_prefix_chars: int = 100,
   230→        log_prefix: str = "",
   231→        providers_allowed: List[str] = None,
   232→        providers_ignored: List[str] = None,
   233→        providers_order: List[str] = None,
   234→        provider_sort: str = None,
   235→        provider_require_parameters: bool = False,
   236→        provider_data_collection: str = None,
   237→        session_id: str = None,
   238→        tool_progress_callback: callable = None,
   239→        thinking_callback: callable = None,
   240→        reasoning_callback: callable = None,
   241→        clarify_callback: callable = None,
   242→        step_callback: callable = None,
   243→        max_tokens: int = None,
   244→        reasoning_config: Dict[str, Any] = None,
   245→        prefill_messages: List[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
   246→        platform: str = None,
   247→        skip_context_files: bool = False,
   248→        skip_memory: bool = False,
   249→        session_db=None,
   250→        honcho_session_key: str = None,
   251→        honcho_manager=None,
   252→        honcho_config=None,
   253→        iteration_budget: "IterationBudget" = None,
   254→        fallback_model: Dict[str, Any] = None,
   255→        checkpoints_enabled: bool = False,
   256→        checkpoint_max_snapshots: int = 50,
   257→        pass_session_id: bool = False,
   258→    ):
   259→        """
   260→        Initialize the AI Agent.
   261→
   262→        Args:
   263→            base_url (str): Base URL for the model API (optional)
   264→            api_key (str): API key for authentication (optional, uses env var if not provided)
   265→            provider (str): Provider identifier (optional; used for telemetry/routing hints)
   266→            api_mode (str): API mode override: "chat_completions" or "codex_responses"
   267→            model (str): Model name to use (default: "anthropic/claude-opus-4.6")
   268→            max_iterations (int): Maximum number of tool calling iterations (default: 90)
   269→            tool_delay (float): Delay between tool calls in seconds (default: 1.0)
   270→            enabled_toolsets (List[str]): Only enable tools from these toolsets (optional)
   271→            disabled_toolsets (List[str]): Disable tools from these toolsets (optional)
   272→            save_trajectories (bool): Whether to save conversation trajectories to JSONL files (default: False)
   273→            verbose_logging (bool): Enable verbose logging for debugging (default: False)
   274→            quiet_mode (bool): Suppress progress output for clean CLI experience (default: False)
   275→            ephemeral_system_prompt (str): System prompt used during agent execution but NOT saved to trajectories (optional)
   276→            log_prefix_chars (int): Number of characters to show in log previews for tool calls/responses (default: 100)
   277→            log_prefix (str): Prefix to add to all log messages for identification in parallel processing (default: "")
   278→            providers_allowed (List[str]): OpenRouter providers to allow (optional)
   279→            providers_ignored (List[str]): OpenRouter providers to ignore (optional)
   280→            providers_order (List[str]): OpenRouter providers to try in order (optional)
   281→            provider_sort (str): Sort providers by price/throughput/latency (optional)
   282→            session_id (str): Pre-generated session ID for logging (optional, auto-generated if not provided)
   283→            tool_progress_callback (callable): Callback function(tool_name, args_preview) for progress notifications
   284→            clarify_callback (callable): Callback function(question, choices) -> str for interactive user questions.
   285→                Provided by the platform layer (CLI or gateway). If None, the clarify tool returns an error.
   286→            max_tokens (int): Maximum tokens for model responses (optional, uses model default if not set)
   287→            reasoning_config (Dict): OpenRouter reasoning configuration override (e.g. {"effort": "none"} to disable thinking).
   288→                If None, defaults to {"enabled": True, "effort": "medium"} for OpenRouter. Set to disable/customize reasoning.
   289→            prefill_messages (List[Dict]): Messages to prepend to conversation history as prefilled context.
   290→                Useful for injecting a few-shot example or priming the model's response style.
   291→                Example: [{"role": "user", "content": "Hi!"}, {"role": "assistant", "content": "Hello!"}]
   292→            platform (str): The interface platform the user is on (e.g. "cli", "telegram", "discord", "whatsapp").
   293→                Used to inject platform-specific formatting hints into the system prompt.
   294→            skip_context_files (bool): If True, skip auto-injection of SOUL.md, AGENTS.md, and .cursorrules
   295→                into the system prompt. Use this for batch processing and data generation to avoid
   296→                polluting trajectories with user-specific persona or project instructions.
   297→            honcho_session_key (str): Session key for Honcho integration (e.g., "telegram:123456" or CLI session_id).
   298→                When provided and Honcho is enabled in config, enables persistent cross-session user modeling.
   299→            honcho_manager: Optional shared HonchoSessionManager owned by the caller.
   300→            honcho_config: Optional HonchoClientConfig corresponding to honcho_manager.
   301→        """
   302→        self.model = model
   303→        self.max_iterations = max_iterations
   304→        # Shared iteration budget — parent creates, children inherit.
   305→        # Consumed by every LLM turn across parent + all subagents.
   306→        self.iteration_budget = iteration_budget or IterationBudget(max_iterations)
   307→        self.tool_delay = tool_delay
   308→        self.save_trajectories = save_trajectories
   309→        self.verbose_logging = verbose_logging
   310→        self.quiet_mode = quiet_mode
   311→        self.ephemeral_system_prompt = ephemeral_system_prompt
   312→        self.platform = platform  # "cli", "telegram", "discord", "whatsapp", etc.
   313→        self.skip_context_files = skip_context_files
   314→        self.pass_session_id = pass_session_id
   315→        self.log_prefix_chars = log_prefix_chars
   316→        self.log_prefix = f"{log_prefix} " if log_prefix else ""
   317→        # Store effective base URL for feature detection (prompt caching, reasoning, etc.)
   318→        # When no base_url is provided, the client defaults to OpenRouter, so reflect that here.
   319→        self.base_url = base_url or OPENROUTER_BASE_URL
   320→        provider_name = provider.strip().lower() if isinstance(provider, str) and provider.strip() else None
   321→        self.provider = provider_name or "openrouter"
   322→        if api_mode in {"chat_completions", "codex_responses", "anthropic_messages"}:
   323→            self.api_mode = api_mode
   324→        elif self.provider == "openai-codex":
   325→            self.api_mode = "codex_responses"
   326→        elif (provider_name is None) and "chatgpt.com/backend-api/codex" in self.base_url.lower():
   327→            self.api_mode = "codex_responses"
   328→            self.provider = "openai-codex"
   329→        elif self.provider == "anthropic" or (provider_name is None and "api.anthropic.com" in self.base_url.lower()):
   330→            self.api_mode = "anthropic_messages"
   331→            self.provider = "anthropic"
   332→        else:
   333→            self.api_mode = "chat_completions"
   334→
   335→        # Remember the original model for switch_model tool guidance
   336→        self._original_model = {"provider": self.provider, "model": self.model}
   337→
   338→        self.tool_progress_callback = tool_progress_callback
   339→        self.thinking_callback = thinking_callback
   340→        self.reasoning_callback = reasoning_callback
   341→        self.clarify_callback = clarify_callback
   342→        self.step_callback = step_callback
   343→        self._last_reported_tool = None  # Track for "new tool" mode
   344→        
   345→        # Interrupt mechanism for breaking out of tool loops
   346→        self._interrupt_requested = False
   347→        self._interrupt_message = None  # Optional message that triggered interrupt
   348→        
   349→        # Subagent delegation state
   350→        self._delegate_depth = 0        # 0 = top-level agent, incremented for children
   351→        self._active_children = []      # Running child AIAgents (for interrupt propagation)
   352→        
   353→        # Store OpenRouter provider preferences
   354→        self.providers_allowed = providers_allowed
   355→        self.providers_ignored = providers_ignored
   356→        self.providers_order = providers_order
   357→        self.provider_sort = provider_sort
   358→        self.provider_require_parameters = provider_require_parameters
   359→        self.provider_data_collection = provider_data_collection
   360→
   361→        # Store toolset filtering options
   362→        self.enabled_toolsets = enabled_toolsets
   363→        self.disabled_toolsets = disabled_toolsets
   364→        
   365→        # Model response configuration
   366→        self.max_tokens = max_tokens  # None = use model default
   367→        self.reasoning_config = reasoning_config  # None = use default (medium for OpenRouter)
   368→        self.prefill_messages = prefill_messages or []  # Prefilled conversation turns
   369→        
   370→        # Anthropic prompt caching: auto-enabled for Claude models via OpenRouter.
   371→        # Reduces input costs by ~75% on multi-turn conversations by caching the
   372→        # conversation prefix. Uses system_and_3 strategy (4 breakpoints).
   373→        is_openrouter = "openrouter" in self.base_url.lower()
   374→        is_claude = "claude" in self.model.lower()
   375→        is_native_anthropic = self.api_mode == "anthropic_messages"
   376→        self._use_prompt_caching = (is_openrouter and is_claude) or is_native_anthropic
   377→        self._cache_ttl = "5m"  # Default 5-minute TTL (1.25x write cost)
   378→        
   379→        # Iteration budget pressure: warn the LLM as it approaches max_iterations.
   380→        # Warnings are injected into the last tool result JSON (not as separate
   381→        # messages) so they don't break message structure or invalidate caching.
   382→        self._budget_caution_threshold = 0.7   # 70% — nudge to start wrapping up
   383→        self._budget_warning_threshold = 0.9   # 90% — urgent, respond now
   384→        self._budget_pressure_enabled = True
   385→
   386→        # Persistent error log -- always writes WARNING+ to ~/.hermes/logs/errors.log
   387→        # so tool failures, API errors, etc. are inspectable after the fact.
   388→        from agent.redact import RedactingFormatter

   390→        _error_log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

   395→        )
   396→        _error_file_handler.setLevel(logging.WARNING)

   399→        ))
   400→        logging.getLogger().addHandler(_error_file_handler)
   401→
   402→        if self.verbose_logging:
   403→            logging.basicConfig(

   405→                format='%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s',
   406→                datefmt='%H:%M:%S'

   408→            for handler in logging.getLogger().handlers:

   412→                ))
   413→            # Keep third-party libraries at WARNING level to reduce noise
   414→            # We have our own retry and error logging that's more informative
   415→            logging.getLogger('openai').setLevel(logging.WARNING)

   426→            logger.info("Verbose logging enabled (third-party library logs suppressed)")
   427→        else:
```

```
   772→    def _max_tokens_param(self, value: int) -> dict:
   773→        """Return the correct max tokens kwarg for the current provider.
   774→        
   775→        OpenAI's newer models (gpt-4o, o-series, gpt-5+) require
   776→        'max_completion_tokens'. OpenRouter, local models, and older
   777→        OpenAI models use 'max_tokens'.
   778→        """
   779→        _is_direct_openai = (
   780→            "api.openai.com" in self.base_url.lower()
   781→            and "openrouter" not in self.base_url.lower()
   782→        )
   783→        if _is_direct_openai:
   784→            return {"max_completion_tokens": value}
   785→        return {"max_tokens": value}
   786→
   787→    def _has_content_after_think_block(self, content: str) -> bool:
   788→        """
   789→        Check if content has actual text after any <think></think> blocks.
   790→        
   791→        This detects cases where the model only outputs reasoning but no actual
   792→        response, which indicates an incomplete generation that should be retried.
   793→        
   794→        Args:
   795→            content: The assistant message content to check
   796→            
   797→        Returns:
   798→            True if there's meaningful content after think blocks, False otherwise
   799→        """
   800→        if not content:
   801→            return False
   802→        
   803→        # Remove all <think>...</think> blocks (including nested ones, non-greedy)
   804→        cleaned = re.sub(r'<think>.*?</think>', '', content, flags=re.DOTALL)
   805→        
   806→        # Check if there's any non-whitespace content remaining
   807→        return bool(cleaned.strip())
   808→    
   809→    def _strip_think_blocks(self, content: str) -> str:
   810→        """Remove <think>...</think> blocks from content, returning only visible text."""
   811→        if not content:
   812→            return ""
   813→        return re.sub(r'<think>.*?</think>', '', content, flags=re.DOTALL)
   814→
   815→    def _looks_like_codex_intermediate_ack(
   816→        self,
   817→        user_message: str,
   818→        assistant_content: str,
   819→        messages: List[Dict[str, Any]],
   820→    ) -> bool:
   821→        """Detect a planning/ack message that should continue instead of ending the turn."""
   822→        if any(isinstance(msg, dict) and msg.get("role") == "tool" for msg in messages):
   823→            return False
   824→
   825→        assistant_text = self._strip_think_blocks(assistant_content or "").strip().lower()
   826→        if not assistant_text:
   827→            return False
   828→        if len(assistant_text) > 1200:
   829→            return False
   830→
   831→        has_future_ack = bool(
   832→            re.search(r"\b(i['’]ll|i will|let me|i can do that|i can help with that)\b", assistant_text)
   833→        )
   834→        if not has_future_ack:
   835→            return False
   836→
   837→        action_markers = (
   838→            "look into",
   839→            "look at",
   840→            "inspect",
   841→            "scan",
   842→            "check",
   843→            "analyz",
   844→            "review",
   845→            "explore",
   846→            "read",
   847→            "open",
   848→            "run",
   849→            "test",
   850→            "fix",
   851→            "debug",
   852→            "search",
   853→            "find",
   854→            "walkthrough",
   855→            "report back",
   856→            "summarize",
   857→        )
   858→        workspace_markers = (
   859→            "directory",
   860→            "current directory",
   861→            "current dir",
   862→            "cwd",
   863→            "repo",
   864→            "repository",
   865→            "codebase",
   866→            "project",
   867→            "folder",
   868→            "filesystem",
   869→            "file tree",
   870→            "files",
   871→            "path",
   872→        )
   873→
   874→        user_text = (user_message or "").strip().lower()
   875→        user_targets_workspace = (
   876→            any(marker in user_text for marker in workspace_markers)
   877→            or "~/" in user_text
   878→            or "/" in user_text
   879→        )
   880→        assistant_mentions_action = any(marker in assistant_text for marker in action_markers)
   881→        assistant_targets_workspace = any(
   882→            marker in assistant_text for marker in workspace_markers
   883→        )
   884→        return (user_targets_workspace or assistant_targets_workspace) and assistant_mentions_action
   885→    
   886→    
   887→    def _extract_reasoning(self, assistant_message) -> Optional[str]:
   888→        """
   889→        Extract reasoning/thinking content from an assistant message.
   890→        
   891→        OpenRouter and various providers can return reasoning in multiple formats:
   892→        1. message.reasoning - Direct reasoning field (DeepSeek, Qwen, etc.)
   893→        2. message.reasoning_content - Alternative field (Moonshot AI, Novita, etc.)
   894→        3. message.reasoning_details - Array of {type, summary, ...} objects (OpenRouter unified)
   895→        
   896→        Args:
   897→            assistant_message: The assistant message object from the API response
   898→            
   899→        Returns:
   900→            Combined reasoning text, or None if no reasoning found
   901→        """
   902→        reasoning_parts = []
   903→        
   904→        # Check direct reasoning field
   905→        if hasattr(assistant_message, 'reasoning') and assistant_message.reasoning:
   906→            reasoning_parts.append(assistant_message.reasoning)
   907→        
   908→        # Check reasoning_content field (alternative name used by some providers)
   909→        if hasattr(assistant_message, 'reasoning_content') and assistant_message.reasoning_content:
   910→            # Don't duplicate if same as reasoning
   911→            if assistant_message.reasoning_content not in reasoning_parts:
   912→                reasoning_parts.append(assistant_message.reasoning_content)
   913→        
   914→        # Check reasoning_details array (OpenRouter unified format)
   915→        # Format: [{"type": "reasoning.summary", "summary": "...", ...}, ...]
   916→        if hasattr(assistant_message, 'reasoning_details') and assistant_message.reasoning_details:
   917→            for detail in assistant_message.reasoning_details:
   918→                if isinstance(detail, dict):
   919→                    # Extract summary from reasoning detail object
   920→                    summary = detail.get('summary') or detail.get('content') or detail.get('text')
   921→                    if summary and summary not in reasoning_parts:
   922→                        reasoning_parts.append(summary)
   923→        
   924→        # Combine all reasoning parts
   925→        if reasoning_parts:
   926→            return "\n\n".join(reasoning_parts)
   927→        
   928→        return None
   929→    
   930→    def _cleanup_task_resources(self, task_id: str) -> None:
   931→        """Clean up VM and browser resources for a given task."""
   932→        try:
   933→            cleanup_vm(task_id)
   934→        except Exception as e:
   935→            if self.verbose_logging:
   936→                logging.warning(f"Failed to cleanup VM for task {task_id}: {e}")
   937→        try:
   938→            cleanup_browser(task_id)

   943→    def _persist_session(self, messages: List[Dict], conversation_history: List[Dict] = None):
   944→        """Save session state to both JSON log and SQLite on any exit path.
   945→
   946→        Ensures conversations are never lost, even on errors or early returns.
   947→        """
   948→        self._session_messages = messages
   949→        self._save_session_log(messages)
   950→        self._flush_messages_to_session_db(messages, conversation_history)
   951→
   952→    def _flush_messages_to_session_db(self, messages: List[Dict], conversation_history: List[Dict] = None):
   953→        """Persist any un-flushed messages to the SQLite session store.
   954→
   955→        Uses _last_flushed_db_idx to track which messages have already been
   956→        written, so repeated calls (from multiple exit paths) only write
   957→        truly new messages — preventing the duplicate-write bug (#860).
   958→        """
   959→        if not self._session_db:
   960→            return
   961→        try:
   962→            start_idx = len(conversation_history) if conversation_history else 0
   963→            flush_from = max(start_idx, self._last_flushed_db_idx)
   964→            for msg in messages[flush_from:]:
   965→                role = msg.get("role", "unknown")
   966→                content = msg.get("content")
   967→                tool_calls_data = None
   968→                if hasattr(msg, "tool_calls") and msg.tool_calls:
   969→                    tool_calls_data = [
   970→                        {"name": tc.function.name, "arguments": tc.function.arguments}
   971→                        for tc in msg.tool_calls
   972→                    ]
   973→                elif isinstance(msg.get("tool_calls"), list):
   974→                    tool_calls_data = msg["tool_calls"]
   975→                self._session_db.append_message(
   976→                    session_id=self.session_id,
   977→                    role=role,
   978→                    content=content,
   979→                    tool_name=msg.get("tool_name"),
   980→                    tool_calls=tool_calls_data,
   981→                    tool_call_id=msg.get("tool_call_id"),
   982→                    finish_reason=msg.get("finish_reason"),
   983→                )
   984→            self._last_flushed_db_idx = len(messages)
   985→        except Exception as e:
   986→            logger.debug("Session DB append_message failed: %s", e)
   987→
   988→    def _get_messages_up_to_last_assistant(self, messages: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
   989→        """
   990→        Get messages up to (but not including) the last assistant turn.
   991→        
   992→        This is used when we need to "roll back" to the last successful point
   993→        in the conversation, typically when the final assistant message is
   994→        incomplete or malformed.
   995→        
   996→        Args:
   997→            messages: Full message list
```

```
2670→    def _switch_model(self, provider: str, model: str) -> dict:
  2671→        """Switch the active model/provider in-place.

  2688→                    "error": f"Provider '{provider}' is not configured. "

  2690→                    "current": {"provider": old_provider, "model": old_model},

  2692→

  2699→            logging.info(
  2700→                "Model switched: %s → %s (%s)",
  2701→                old_model, model, provider,
  2702→            )
  2703→            return {
  2704→                "success": True,
  2705→                "previous": {"provider": old_provider, "model": old_model},
  2706→                "current": {"provider": provider, "model": model},
  2707→                "message": "Model switched successfully",
```

```
3211→    def _invoke_tool(self, function_name: str, function_args: dict, effective_task_id: str) -> str:

  3213→

  3215→        tools. Used by the concurrent execution path; the sequential path retains
  3216→        its own inline invocation for backward-compatible display handling.

  3218→        if function_name == "todo":

  3222→                merge=function_args.get("merge", False),

  3292→    def _execute_tool_calls_concurrent(self, assistant_message, messages: list, effective_task_id: str, api_call_count: int = 0) -> None:
  3293→        """Execute multiple tool calls concurrently using a thread pool.
  3294→
  3295→        Results are collected in the original tool-call order and appended to
  3296→        messages so the API sees them in the expected sequence.
  3297→        """
  3298→        tool_calls = assistant_message.tool_calls
  3299→        num_tools = len(tool_calls)
  3300→
  3301→        # ── Pre-flight: interrupt check ──────────────────────────────────
  3302→        if self._interrupt_requested:
  3303→            print(f"{self.log_prefix}⚡ Interrupt: skipping {num_tools} tool call(s)")
  3304→            for tc in tool_calls:
  3305→                messages.append({
  3306→                    "role": "tool",
  3307→                    "content": f"[Tool execution cancelled — {tc.function.name} was skipped due to user interrupt]",
  3308→                    "tool_call_id": tc.id,
  3309→                })
  3310→            return
  3311→
  3312→        # ── State-mutating tools: force sequential to prevent races ──────
  3313→        if any(tc.function.name == "switch_model" for tc in tool_calls):
  3314→            return self._execute_tool_calls_sequential(
  3315→                assistant_message, messages, effective_task_id, api_call_count
  3316→            )
  3317→
  3318→        # ── Parse args + pre-execution bookkeeping ───────────────────────
  3319→        parsed_calls = []  # list of (tool_call, function_name, function_args)
  3320→        for tool_call in tool_calls:
  3321→            function_name = tool_call.function.name
  3322→
  3323→            # Reset nudge counters
  3324→            if function_name == "memory":
  3325→                self._turns_since_memory = 0
  3326→            elif function_name == "skill_manage":
  3327→                self._iters_since_skill = 0
  3328→
  3329→            try:
  3330→                function_args = json.loads(tool_call.function.arguments)
  3331→            except json.JSONDecodeError:
  3332→                function_args = {}
  3333→            if not isinstance(function_args, dict):
  3334→                function_args = {}
  3335→
  3336→            # Checkpoint for file-mutating tools
  3337→            if function_name in ("write_file", "patch") and self._checkpoint_mgr.enabled:
  3338→                try:
  3339→                    file_path = function_args.get("path", "")
  3340→                    if file_path:
  3341→                        work_dir = self._checkpoint_mgr.get_working_dir_for_path(file_path)
  3342→                        self._checkpoint_mgr.ensure_checkpoint(work_dir, f"before {function_name}")
  3343→                except Exception:
  3344→                    pass
  3345→
  3346→            parsed_calls.append((tool_call, function_name, function_args))
  3347→
  3348→        # ── Logging / callbacks ──────────────────────────────────────────
  3349→        tool_names_str = ", ".join(name for _, name, _ in parsed_calls)
  3350→        if not self.quiet_mode:
  3351→            print(f"  ⚡ Concurrent: {num_tools} tool calls — {tool_names_str}")
  3352→            for i, (tc, name, args) in enumerate(parsed_calls, 1):
  3353→                args_str = json.dumps(args, ensure_ascii=False)
  3354→                args_preview = args_str[:self.log_prefix_chars] + "..." if len(args_str) > self.log_prefix_chars else args_str
  3355→                print(f"  📞 Tool {i}: {name}({list(args.keys())}) - {args_preview}")
  3356→
  3357→        for _, name, args in parsed_calls:
  3358→            if self.tool_progress_callback:
  3359→                try:
  3360→                    preview = _build_tool_preview(name, args)
  3361→                    self.tool_progress_callback(name, preview, args)
  3362→                except Exception as cb_err:
  3363→                    logging.debug(f"Tool progress callback error: {cb_err}")
  3364→
  3365→        # ── Concurrent execution ─────────────────────────────────────────
  3366→        # Each slot holds (function_name, function_args, function_result, duration, error_flag)
  3367→        results = [None] * num_tools
  3368→
  3369→        def _run_tool(index, tool_call, function_name, function_args):
  3370→            """Worker function executed in a thread."""
  3371→            start = time.time()
  3372→            try:
  3373→                result = self._invoke_tool(function_name, function_args, effective_task_id)
  3374→            except Exception as tool_error:
  3375→                result = f"Error executing tool '{function_name}': {tool_error}"
  3376→                logger.error("_invoke_tool raised for %s: %s", function_name, tool_error, exc_info=True)
  3377→            duration = time.time() - start
  3378→            is_error, _ = _detect_tool_failure(function_name, result)
  3379→            results[index] = (function_name, function_args, result, duration, is_error)
  3380→
  3381→        # Start spinner for CLI mode
  3382→        spinner = None
  3383→        if self.quiet_mode:
  3384→            face = random.choice(KawaiiSpinner.KAWAII_WAITING)
  3385→            spinner = KawaiiSpinner(f"{face} ⚡ running {num_tools} tools concurrently", spinner_type='dots')
  3386→            spinner.start()
  3387→
  3388→        try:
  3389→            max_workers = min(num_tools, _MAX_TOOL_WORKERS)
  3390→            with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers) as executor:
  3391→                futures = []
  3392→                for i, (tc, name, args) in enumerate(parsed_calls):
  3393→                    f = executor.submit(_run_tool, i, tc, name, args)
  3394→                    futures.append(f)
  3395→
  3396→                # Wait for all to complete (exceptions are captured inside _run_tool)
  3397→                concurrent.futures.wait(futures)
  3398→        finally:
  3399→            if spinner:

  3401→                completed = sum(1 for r in results if r is not None)
  3402→                total_dur = sum(r[3] for r in results if r is not None)
  3403→                spinner.stop(f"⚡ {completed}/{num_tools} tools completed in {total_dur:.1f}s total")
  3404→
  3405→        # ── Post-execution: display per-tool results ─────────────────────
  3406→        for i, (tc, name, args) in enumerate(parsed_calls):
  3407→            r = results[i]
  3408→            if r is None:

  3410→                function_result = f"Error executing tool '{name}': thread did not return a result"
```

```
1→"""Shared auxiliary OpenAI client for cheap/fast side tasks.
     2→
     3→Provides a single resolution chain so every consumer (context compression,
     4→session search, web extraction, vision analysis, browser vision) picks up
     5→the best available backend without duplicating fallback logic.
     6→
     7→Resolution order for text tasks (auto mode):
     8→  1. OpenRouter  (OPENROUTER_API_KEY)
     9→  2. Nous Portal (~/.hermes/auth.json active provider)
    10→  3. Custom endpoint (OPENAI_BASE_URL + OPENAI_API_KEY)
    11→  4. Codex OAuth (Responses API via chatgpt.com with gpt-5.3-codex,
    12→     wrapped to look like a chat.completions client)
    13→  5. Direct API-key providers (z.ai/GLM, Kimi/Moonshot, MiniMax, MiniMax-CN)
    14→     — checked via PROVIDER_REGISTRY entries with auth_type='api_key'
    15→  6. None
    16→
    17→Resolution order for vision/multimodal tasks (auto mode):
    18→  1. OpenRouter
    19→  2. Nous Portal
    20→  3. Codex OAuth (gpt-5.3-codex supports vision via Responses API)
    21→  4. Custom endpoint (for local vision models: Qwen-VL, LLaVA, Pixtral, etc.)
    22→  5. None  (API-key providers like z.ai/Kimi/MiniMax are skipped —
    23→     they may not support multimodal)
    24→
    25→Per-task provider overrides (e.g. AUXILIARY_VISION_PROVIDER,
    26→CONTEXT_COMPRESSION_PROVIDER) can force a specific provider for each task:
    27→"openrouter", "nous", "codex", or "main" (= steps 3-5).
    28→Default "auto" follows the chains above.
    29→
    30→Per-task model overrides (e.g. AUXILIARY_VISION_MODEL,
    31→AUXILIARY_WEB_EXTRACT_MODEL) let callers use a different model slug
    32→than the provider's default.
    33→"""
    34→
    35→import json
    36→import logging
    37→import os
    38→from pathlib import Path
    39→from types import SimpleNamespace
    40→from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
    41→
    42→from openai import OpenAI
    43→
    44→from hermes_constants import OPENROUTER_BASE_URL
    45→
    46→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    47→
    48→# Default auxiliary models for direct API-key providers (cheap/fast for side tasks)
    49→_API_KEY_PROVIDER_AUX_MODELS: Dict[str, str] = {
    50→    "zai": "glm-4.5-flash",
    51→    "kimi-coding": "kimi-k2-turbo-preview",
    52→    "minimax": "MiniMax-M2.5-highspeed",
    53→    "minimax-cn": "MiniMax-M2.5-highspeed",
    54→    "anthropic": "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001",
    55→}
    56→
    57→# OpenRouter app attribution headers
    58→_OR_HEADERS = {
    59→    "HTTP-Referer": "https://hermes-agent.nousresearch.com",
    60→    "X-OpenRouter-Title": "Hermes Agent",
    61→    "X-OpenRouter-Categories": "productivity,cli-agent",
    62→}
    63→
    64→# Nous Portal extra_body for product attribution.
    65→# Callers should pass this as extra_body in chat.completions.create()
    66→# when the auxiliary client is backed by Nous Portal.
    67→NOUS_EXTRA_BODY = {"tags": ["product=hermes-agent"]}
    68→
    69→# Set at resolve time — True if the auxiliary client points to Nous Portal
    70→auxiliary_is_nous: bool = False
    71→
    72→# Default auxiliary models per provider
    73→_OPENROUTER_MODEL = "google/gemini-3-flash-preview"
    74→_NOUS_MODEL = "gemini-3-flash"
    75→_NOUS_DEFAULT_BASE_URL = "https://inference-api.nousresearch.com/v1"
    76→_AUTH_JSON_PATH = Path.home() / ".hermes" / "auth.json"
    77→
    78→# Codex fallback: uses the Responses API (the only endpoint the Codex
    79→# OAuth token can access) with a fast model for auxiliary tasks.
    80→_CODEX_AUX_MODEL = "gpt-5.3-codex"
    81→_CODEX_AUX_BASE_URL = "https://chatgpt.com/backend-api/codex"
    82→
    83→
    84→# ── Codex Responses → chat.completions adapter ─────────────────────────────
    85→# All auxiliary consumers call client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs) and
    86→# read response.choices[0].message.content. This adapter translates those
    87→# calls to the Codex Responses API so callers don't need any changes.
    88→
    89→
    90→def _convert_content_for_responses(content: Any) -> Any:
    91→    """Convert chat.completions content to Responses API format.
    92→
    93→    chat.completions uses:
    94→      {"type": "text", "text": "..."}
    95→      {"type": "image_url", "image_url": {"url": "data:image/png;base64,..."}}
    96→
    97→    Responses API uses:
    98→      {"type": "input_text", "text": "..."}
    99→      {"type": "input_image", "image_url": "data:image/png;base64,..."}
   100→
   101→    If content is a plain string, it's returned as-is (the Responses API
   102→    accepts strings directly for text-only messages).
   103→    """
   104→    if isinstance(content, str):
   105→        return content
   106→    if not isinstance(content, list):
   107→        return str(content) if content else ""
   108→
   109→    converted: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   110→    for part in content:
   111→        if not isinstance(part, dict):
   112→            continue
   113→        ptype = part.get("type", "")
   114→        if ptype == "text":
   115→            converted.append({"type": "input_text", "text": part.get("text", "")})
   116→        elif ptype == "image_url":
   117→            # chat.completions nests the URL: {"image_url": {"url": "..."}}
   118→            image_data = part.get("image_url", {})
   119→            url = image_data.get("url", "") if isinstance(image_data, dict) else str(image_data)
   120→            entry: Dict[str, Any] = {"type": "input_image", "image_url": url}
   121→            # Preserve detail if specified
   122→            detail = image_data.get("detail") if isinstance(image_data, dict) else None
   123→            if detail:
   124→                entry["detail"] = detail
   125→            converted.append(entry)
   126→        elif ptype in ("input_text", "input_image"):
   127→            # Already in Responses format — pass through
   128→            converted.append(part)
   129→        else:
   130→            # Unknown content type — try to preserve as text
   131→            text = part.get("text", "")
   132→            if text:
   133→                converted.append({"type": "input_text", "text": text})
   134→
   135→    return converted or ""
   136→
   137→
   138→class _CodexCompletionsAdapter:
   139→    """Drop-in shim that accepts chat.completions.create() kwargs and
   140→    routes them through the Codex Responses streaming API."""
   141→
   142→    def __init__(self, real_client: OpenAI, model: str):
   143→        self._client = real_client
   144→        self._model = model
   145→
   146→    def create(self, **kwargs) -> Any:
   147→        messages = kwargs.get("messages", [])
   148→        model = kwargs.get("model", self._model)
   149→        temperature = kwargs.get("temperature")
   150→
   151→        # Separate system/instructions from conversation messages.
   152→        # Convert chat.completions multimodal content blocks to Responses
   153→        # API format (input_text / input_image instead of text / image_url).
   154→        instructions = "You are a helpful assistant."
   155→        input_msgs: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   156→        for msg in messages:
   157→            role = msg.get("role", "user")
   158→            content = msg.get("content") or ""
   159→            if role == "system":
   160→                instructions = content if isinstance(content, str) else str(content)
   161→            else:
   162→                input_msgs.append({
   163→                    "role": role,
   164→                    "content": _convert_content_for_responses(content),
   165→                })
   166→
   167→        resp_kwargs: Dict[str, Any] = {
   168→            "model": model,
   169→            "instructions": instructions,
   170→            "input": input_msgs or [{"role": "user", "content": ""}],
   171→            "store": False,
   172→        }
   173→
   174→        # Note: the Codex endpoint (chatgpt.com/backend-api/codex) does NOT
   175→        # support max_output_tokens or temperature — omit to avoid 400 errors.
   176→
   177→        # Tools support for flush_memories and similar callers
   178→        tools = kwargs.get("tools")
   179→        if tools:
   180→            converted = []
   181→            for t in tools:
   182→                fn = t.get("function", {}) if isinstance(t, dict) else {}
   183→                name = fn.get("name")
   184→                if not name:
   185→                    continue
   186→                converted.append({
   187→                    "type": "function",
   188→                    "name": name,
   189→                    "description": fn.get("description", ""),
   190→                    "parameters": fn.get("parameters", {}),
   191→                })
   192→            if converted:
   193→                resp_kwargs["tools"] = converted
   194→
   195→        # Stream and collect the response
   196→        text_parts: List[str] = []
   197→        tool_calls_raw: List[Any] = []
   198→        usage = None
   199→
   200→        try:
   201→            with self._client.responses.stream(**resp_kwargs) as stream:
   202→                for _event in stream:
   203→                    pass
   204→                final = stream.get_final_response()
   205→
   206→            # Extract text and tool calls from the Responses output
   207→            for item in getattr(final, "output", []):
   208→                item_type = getattr(item, "type", None)
   209→                if item_type == "message":
   210→                    for part in getattr(item, "content", []):
   211→                        ptype = getattr(part, "type", None)
   212→                        if ptype in ("output_text", "text"):
   213→                            text_parts.append(getattr(part, "text", ""))
   214→                elif item_type == "function_call":
   215→                    tool_calls_raw.append(SimpleNamespace(
   216→                        id=getattr(item, "call_id", ""),
   217→                        type="function",
   218→                        function=SimpleNamespace(
   219→                            name=getattr(item, "name", ""),
   220→                            arguments=getattr(item, "arguments", "{}"),
   221→                        ),
   222→                    ))
   223→
   224→            resp_usage = getattr(final, "usage", None)
   225→            if resp_usage:
   226→                usage = SimpleNamespace(
   227→                    prompt_tokens=getattr(resp_usage, "input_tokens", 0),
   228→                    completion_tokens=getattr(resp_usage, "output_tokens", 0),
   229→                    total_tokens=getattr(resp_usage, "total_tokens", 0),
   230→                )
   231→        except Exception as exc:
   232→            logger.debug("Codex auxiliary Responses API call failed: %s", exc)
   233→            raise
   234→
   235→        content = "".join(text_parts).strip() or None
   236→
   237→        # Build a response that looks like chat.completions
   238→        message = SimpleNamespace(
   239→            role="assistant",
   240→            content=content,
   241→            tool_calls=tool_calls_raw or None,
   242→        )
   243→        choice = SimpleNamespace(
   244→            index=0,
   245→            message=message,
   246→            finish_reason="stop" if not tool_calls_raw else "tool_calls",
   247→        )
   248→        return SimpleNamespace(
   249→            choices=[choice],
   250→            model=model,
   251→            usage=usage,
   252→        )
   253→
   254→
   255→class _CodexChatShim:
   256→    """Wraps the adapter to provide client.chat.completions.create()."""
   257→
   258→    def __init__(self, adapter: _CodexCompletionsAdapter):
   259→        self.completions = adapter
   260→
   261→
   262→class CodexAuxiliaryClient:
   263→    """OpenAI-client-compatible wrapper that routes through Codex Responses API.
   264→
   265→    Consumers can call client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs) as normal.
   266→    Also exposes .api_key and .base_url for introspection by async wrappers.
   267→    """
   268→
   269→    def __init__(self, real_client: OpenAI, model: str):
   270→        self._real_client = real_client
   271→        adapter = _CodexCompletionsAdapter(real_client, model)
   272→        self.chat = _CodexChatShim(adapter)
   273→        self.[REDACTED]
   274→        self.base_url = real_client.base_url
   275→
   276→    def close(self):
   277→        self._real_client.close()
   278→
   279→
   280→class _AsyncCodexCompletionsAdapter:
   281→    """Async version of the Codex Responses adapter.
   282→
   283→    Wraps the sync adapter via asyncio.to_thread() so async consumers
   284→    (web_tools, session_search) can await it as normal.
   285→    """
   286→
   287→    def __init__(self, sync_adapter: _CodexCompletionsAdapter):
   288→        self._sync = sync_adapter
   289→
   290→    async def create(self, **kwargs) -> Any:
   291→        import asyncio
   292→        return await asyncio.to_thread(self._sync.create, **kwargs)
   293→
   294→
   295→class _AsyncCodexChatShim:
   296→    def __init__(self, adapter: _AsyncCodexCompletionsAdapter):
   297→        self.completions = adapter
   298→
   299→
   300→class AsyncCodexAuxiliaryClient:
```

```
705→def resolve_provider_credentials(
   706→    provider: str,
   707→    model: str,
   708→) -> Optional[dict]:
   709→    """Resolve a provider+model into a complete credentials bundle.
   710→
   711→    Thin wrapper around :func:`resolve_provider_client` that also derives
   712→    ``api_mode`` from the provider name.  Used by every code path that
   713→    needs to construct or switch to a new provider: fallback activation,
   714→    the ``switch_model`` tool, and per-task delegation overrides.
   715→
   716→    Returns a dict on success::
   717→
   718→        {
   719→            "provider": "openrouter",
   720→            "model": "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6",
   721→            "base_url": "https://...",
   722→            "api_key": "sk-...",
   723→            "api_mode": "chat_completions",
   724→            "client": <OpenAI>,       # ready-to-use client object
   725→        }
   726→
   727→    Returns ``None`` if the provider is not configured (no API key).
   728→    """
   729→    client, _ = resolve_provider_client(provider, model=model, raw_codex=True)
   730→    if client is None:
   731→        return None
   732→
   733→    api_mode = "chat_completions"
   734→    if provider == "openai-codex":
   735→        api_mode = "codex_responses"
   736→    elif provider == "anthropic":
   737→        api_mode = "anthropic_messages"
   738→
   739→    return {
   740→        "provider": provider,
   741→        "model": model,
   742→        "base_url": str(client.base_url),
   743→        "api_key": client.api_key,
   744→        "api_mode": api_mode,
   745→        "client": client,
   746→    }
   747→

   751→def get_text_auxiliary_client(task: str = "") -> Tuple[Optional[OpenAI], Optional[str]]:
   752→    """Return (client, default_model_slug) for text-only auxiliary tasks.
   753→
   754→    Args:
   755→        task: Optional task name ("compression", "web_extract") to check
   756→              for a task-specific provider override.
   757→
   758→    Callers may override the returned model with a per-task env var
   759→    (e.g. CONTEXT_COMPRESSION_MODEL, AUXILIARY_WEB_EXTRACT_MODEL).
   760→    """
   761→    forced = _get_auxiliary_provider(task)
   762→    if forced != "auto":
   763→        return resolve_provider_client(forced)
   764→    return resolve_provider_client("auto")
   765→

   767→def get_async_text_auxiliary_client(task: str = ""):
   768→    """Return (async_client, model_slug) for async consumers.
   769→
   770→    For standard providers returns (AsyncOpenAI, model). For Codex returns
   771→    (AsyncCodexAuxiliaryClient, model) which wraps the Responses API.
   772→    Returns (None, None) when no provider is available.
   773→    """
   774→    forced = _get_auxiliary_provider(task)
   775→    if forced != "auto":
   776→        return resolve_provider_client(forced, async_mode=True)
   777→    return resolve_provider_client("auto", async_mode=True)

   780→def get_vision_auxiliary_client() -> Tuple[Optional[OpenAI], Optional[str]]:
   781→    """Return (client, default_model_slug) for vision/multimodal auxiliary tasks.
   782→
   783→    Checks AUXILIARY_VISION_PROVIDER for a forced provider, otherwise
   784→    auto-detects.  Callers may override the returned model with
   785→    AUXILIARY_VISION_MODEL.
   786→
   787→    In auto mode, only providers known to support multimodal are tried:
   788→    OpenRouter, Nous Portal, and Codex OAuth (gpt-5.3-codex supports
   789→    vision via the Responses API).  Custom endpoints and API-key
   790→    providers are skipped — they may not handle vision input.  To use
   791→    them, set AUXILIARY_VISION_PROVIDER explicitly.
   792→    """
   793→    forced = _get_auxiliary_provider("vision")
   794→    if forced != "auto":
   795→        return resolve_provider_client(forced)
   796→    # Auto: try providers known to support multimodal first, then fall
   797→    # back to the user's custom endpoint.  Many local models (Qwen-VL,
   798→    # LLaVA, Pixtral, etc.) support vision — skipping them entirely
   799→    # caused silent failures for local-only users.
   800→    for try_fn in (_try_openrouter, _try_nous, _try_codex,
   801→                   _try_custom_endpoint):
   802→        client, model = try_fn()
   803→        if client is not None:
   804→            return client, model
   805→    logger.debug("Auxiliary vision client: none available")
   806→    return None, None

   833→def auxiliary_max_tokens_param(value: int) -> dict:
   834→    """Return the correct max tokens kwarg for the auxiliary client's provider.
   835→    
   836→    OpenRouter and local models use 'max_tokens'. Direct OpenAI with newer
   837→    models (gpt-4o, o-series, gpt-5+) requires 'max_completion_tokens'.
   838→    The Codex adapter translates max_tokens internally, so we use max_tokens
   839→    for it as well.
   840→    """
   841→    custom_base = os.getenv("OPENAI_BASE_URL", "")
   842→    or_key = os.getenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY")
   843→    # Only use max_completion_tokens for direct OpenAI custom endpoints
   844→    if (not or_key
   845→            and _read_nous_auth() is None
   846→            and "api.openai.com" in custom_base.lower()):
   847→        return {"max_completion_tokens": value}
   848→    return {"max_tokens": value}

   850→
   851→# ── Centralized LLM Call API ────────────────────────────────────────────────
   852→#
   853→# call_llm() and async_call_llm() own the full request lifecycle:
   854→#   1. Resolve provider + model from task config (or explicit args)
   855→#   2. Get or create a cached client for that provider
   856→#   3. Format request args for the provider + model (max_tokens handling, etc.)
   857→#   4. Make the API call
   858→#   5. Return the response
   859→#
   860→# Every auxiliary LLM consumer should use these instead of manually
   861→# constructing clients and calling .chat.completions.create().
   862→
   863→# Client cache: (provider, async_mode) -> (client, default_model)
   864→# Protected by _client_cache_lock for thread safety — subagent threads
   865→# call call_llm() concurrently for compression, session search, etc.
   866→import threading
   867→_client_cache: Dict[tuple, tuple] = {}
   868→_client_cache_lock = threading.Lock()
   869→
   870→
   871→def _get_cached_client(
   872→    provider: str, model: str = None, async_mode: bool = False,
   873→) -> Tuple[Optional[Any], Optional[str]]:
   874→    """Get or create a cached client for the given provider.
   875→
   876→    Thread-safe: multiple subagent threads may resolve clients concurrently.
   877→    """
   878→    cache_key = (provider, async_mode)
   879→    with _client_cache_lock:
   880→        if cache_key in _client_cache:
   881→            cached_client, cached_default = _client_cache[cache_key]
   882→            return cached_client, model or cached_default
   883→    # Build outside the lock — resolve_provider_client can be slow (network)
   884→    client, default_model = resolve_provider_client(provider, model, async_mode)
   885→    if client is not None:
   886→        with _client_cache_lock:
   887→            # Double-check: another thread may have populated it
   888→            if cache_key not in _client_cache:
   889→                _client_cache[cache_key] = (client, default_model)
   890→            else:
   891→                # Use the already-cached client to avoid duplicates
   892→                client, default_model = _client_cache[cache_key]
   893→    return client, model or default_model
```
