```
3908→    def run_conversation(
  3909→        self,
  3910→        user_message: str,
  3911→        system_message: str = None,
  3912→        conversation_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
  3913→        task_id: str = None
  3914→    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
  3915→        """
  3916→        Run a complete conversation with tool calling until completion.
  3917→
  3918→        Args:
  3919→            user_message (str): The user's message/question
  3920→            system_message (str): Custom system message (optional, overrides ephemeral_system_prompt if provided)
  3921→            conversation_history (List[Dict]): Previous conversation messages (optional)
  3922→            task_id (str): Unique identifier for this task to isolate VMs between concurrent tasks (optional, auto-generated if not provided)
  3923→
  3924→        Returns:
  3925→            Dict: Complete conversation result with final response and message history
  3926→        """
  3927→        # Guard stdout against OSError from broken pipes (systemd/headless/daemon).
  3928→        # Installed once, transparent when stdout is healthy, prevents crash on write.
  3929→        if not isinstance(sys.stdout, _SafeWriter):
  3930→            sys.stdout = _SafeWriter(sys.stdout)
  3931→
  3932→        # Generate unique task_id if not provided to isolate VMs between concurrent tasks
  3933→        effective_task_id = task_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
  3934→        
  3935→        # Reset retry counters and iteration budget at the start of each turn
  3936→        # so subagent usage from a previous turn doesn't eat into the next one.
  3937→        self._invalid_tool_retries = 0
  3938→        self._invalid_json_retries = 0
  3939→        self._empty_content_retries = 0
  3940→        self._incomplete_scratchpad_retries = 0
  3941→        self._codex_incomplete_retries = 0
  3942→        self._last_content_with_tools = None
  3943→        self._turns_since_memory = 0
  3944→        self._iters_since_skill = 0
  3945→        self.iteration_budget = IterationBudget(self.max_iterations)
  3946→        
  3947→        # Initialize conversation (copy to avoid mutating the caller's list)
  3948→        messages = list(conversation_history) if conversation_history else []
  3949→        
  3950→        # Hydrate todo store from conversation history (gateway creates a fresh
  3951→        # AIAgent per message, so the in-memory store is empty -- we need to
  3952→        # recover the todo state from the most recent todo tool response in history)
  3953→        if conversation_history and not self._todo_store.has_items():
  3954→            self._hydrate_todo_store(conversation_history)
  3955→        
  3956→        # Prefill messages (few-shot priming) are injected at API-call time only,
  3957→        # never stored in the messages list. This keeps them ephemeral: they won't
  3958→        # be saved to session DB, session logs, or batch trajectories, but they're
  3959→        # automatically re-applied on every API call (including session continuations).
  3960→        
  3961→        # Track user turns for memory flush and periodic nudge logic
  3962→        self._user_turn_count += 1
  3963→
  3964→        # Preserve the original user message before nudge injection.
  3965→        # Honcho should receive the actual user input, not system nudges.
  3966→        original_user_message = user_message
  3967→
  3968→        # Periodic memory nudge: remind the model to consider saving memories.
  3969→        # Counter resets whenever the memory tool is actually used.
  3970→        if (self._memory_nudge_interval > 0
  3971→                and "memory" in self.valid_tool_names
  3972→                and self._memory_store):
  3973→            self._turns_since_memory += 1
  3974→            if self._turns_since_memory >= self._memory_nudge_interval:
  3975→                user_message += (
  3976→                    "\n\n[System: You've had several exchanges in this session. "
  3977→                    "Consider whether there's anything worth saving to your memories.]"
  3978→                )
  3979→                self._turns_since_memory = 0
```

```
2532→    def _interruptible_api_call(self, api_kwargs: dict):
  2533→        """
  2534→        Run the API call in a background thread so the main conversation loop
  2535→        can detect interrupts without waiting for the full HTTP round-trip.
  2536→        
  2537→        On interrupt, closes the HTTP client to cancel the in-flight request
  2538→        (stops token generation and avoids wasting money), then rebuilds the
  2539→        client for future calls.
  2540→        """
  2541→        result = {"response": None, "error": None}
  2542→
  2543→        def _call():
  2544→            try:
  2545→                if self.api_mode == "codex_responses":
  2546→                    result["response"] = self._run_codex_stream(api_kwargs)
  2547→                elif self.api_mode == "anthropic_messages":
  2548→                    result["response"] = self._anthropic_client.messages.create(**api_kwargs)
  2549→                else:
  2550→                    result["response"] = self.client.chat.completions.create(**api_kwargs)
  2551→            except Exception as e:
  2552→                result["error"] = e
  2553→
  2554→        t = threading.Thread(target=_call, daemon=True)
  2555→        t.start()
  2556→        while t.is_alive():
  2557→            t.join(timeout=0.3)
  2558→            if self._interrupt_requested:
  2559→                # Force-close the HTTP connection to stop token generation
  2560→                try:
  2561→                    if self.api_mode == "anthropic_messages":
  2562→                        self._anthropic_client.close()
  2563→                    else:
  2564→                        self.client.close()
  2565→                except Exception:
  2566→                    pass
  2567→                # Rebuild the client for future calls (cheap, no network)
  2568→                try:
  2569→                    if self.api_mode == "anthropic_messages":
  2570→                        from agent.anthropic_adapter import build_anthropic_client
  2571→                        self._anthropic_client = build_anthropic_client(self._anthropic_api_key)
  2572→                    else:
  2573→                        self.client = OpenAI(**self._client_kwargs)
  2574→                except Exception:
  2575→                    pass
  2576→                raise InterruptedError("Agent interrupted during API call")
  2577→        if result["error"] is not None:
  2578→            raise result["error"]
  2579→        return result["response"]
  2580→
  2581→    # ── Provider credential swap ─────────────────────────────────────────
  2582→
  2583→    def _apply_provider_credentials(self, creds: dict) -> None:
  2584→        """Apply a resolved credentials bundle to this agent in-place.
  2585→
  2586→        *creds* is a dict from :func:`resolve_provider_credentials` with
  2587→        keys: provider, model, base_url, api_key, api_mode, client.
  2588→
  2589→        Handles the OpenAI-vs-Anthropic client split and re-evaluates
  2590→        prompt-caching eligibility.
  2591→        """
  2592→        self.model = creds["model"]
  2593→        self.provider = creds["provider"]
  2594→        self.base_url = creds["base_url"]
  2595→        self.api_mode = creds["api_mode"]
  2596→
  2597→        if creds["api_mode"] == "anthropic_messages":
  2598→            from agent.anthropic_adapter import build_anthropic_client, resolve_anthropic_token
  2599→            effective_key = creds["api_key"] or resolve_anthropic_token() or ""
  2600→            self._anthropic_api_key = effective_key
  2601→            self._anthropic_client = build_anthropic_client(effective_key)
  2602→            self.client = None
  2603→            self._client_kwargs = {}
  2604→        else:
  2605→            self.client = creds["client"]
  2606→            self._client_kwargs = {
  2607→                "api_key": creds["api_key"],
  2608→                "base_url": creds["base_url"],
  2609→            }
  2610→
  2611→        # Re-evaluate prompt caching for the new provider/model
  2612→        is_native_anthropic = creds["api_mode"] == "anthropic_messages"
  2613→        self._use_prompt_caching = (
  2614→            ("openrouter" in creds["base_url"].lower()
  2615→             and "claude" in creds["model"].lower())
  2616→            or is_native_anthropic
  2617→        )
  2618→
  2619→    # ── Provider fallback ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2620→
  2621→    def _try_activate_fallback(self) -> bool:
  2622→        """Switch to the configured fallback model/provider.
  2623→
  2624→        Called when the primary model is failing after retries.  Swaps the
  2625→        OpenAI client, model slug, and provider in-place so the retry loop
  2626→        can continue with the new backend.  One-shot: returns False if
  2627→        already activated or not configured.
  2628→
  2629→        Uses the centralized provider router (resolve_provider_client) for
  2630→        auth resolution and client construction — no duplicated provider→key
  2631→        mappings.
```

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me compile my findings into a comprehensive report:

## Comprehensive Architecture Report: LLM Model Selection and Mid-Conversation Switching

Based on my thorough investigation of the Hermes agent codebase, here's a detailed breakdown of how the model is selected, managed, and can be changed during a conversation:

---

### 1. HOW THE MODEL IS INITIALLY SET

**Configuration Sources (Priority Order):**

1. **CLI Flag** - Highest priority
   - `--model` parameter to `hermes chat` or `run_agent.py`
   - Example: `hermes chat --model openrouter:anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5`

2. **Config File** - `.env` or `~/.hermes/.env`
   - Environment variables like `OPENROUTER_API_KEY`, `OPENAI_BASE_URL`, `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`

3. **Hermes Config** - `~/.hermes/config.yaml` 
   - Persistent user preferences

4. **Runtime Resolution** - Provider auto-detection
   - `hermes_cli/runtime_provider.py` resolves credentials for the specified provider

**Default Model:**
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/run_agent.py` line 220
- Default: `"anthropic/claude-opus-4.6"` (OpenRouter format)

---

### 2. WHERE THE MODEL IS STORED AT RUNTIME

The AIAgent class stores model state in multiple attributes:

**Primary Model Container:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/run_agent.py`

```
self.model              # Current model ID (e.g., "anthropic/claude-opus-4.6")
self.provider           # Current provider (e.g., "openrouter", "anthropic")
self.base_url           # API endpoint URL
self.api_mode           # "chat_completions", "codex_responses", or "anthropic_messages"
self._original_model    # {"provider": "...", "model": "..."} - tracks initial model
self._client_kwargs     # {"api_key": "...", "base_url": "..."} - for reconstructing client
```

**Client Objects:**

```
self.client             # OpenAI() instance for standard OpenAI-compatible APIs
self._anthropic_client  # Anthropic() instance for native Anthropic API
```

The agent maintains **both** client objects but only one is active at a time depending on `api_mode`:
- If `api_mode == "anthropic_messages"`: Use `_anthropic_client`, ignore `client`
- Otherwise: Use `client`, ignore `_anthropic_client`

---

### 3. HOW THE AGENT LOOP CALLS THE LLM

**Full Call Flow:**

#### Step A: Build API Arguments
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/run_agent.py` line 4268
- Method: `self._build_api_kwargs(api_messages)`
- Builds kwargs dict with:
  - `model`: `self.model` (the current model)
  - `messages`: conversation history with system prompt
  - `tools`: available tool schemas
  - `temperature`: `self.temperature`
  - `max_tokens`: `self.max_tokens` if set

#### Step B: Make the API Call
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/run_agent.py` line 4275
- Method: `self._interruptible_api_call(api_kwargs)` 
- Actual calls (lines 2545-2550):
  ```python
  if self.api_mode == "codex_responses":
      result["response"] = self._run_codex_stream(api_kwargs)
  elif self.api_mode == "anthropic_messages":
      result["response"] = self._anthropic_client.messages.create(**api_kwargs)
  else:
      result["response"] = self.client.chat.completions.create(**api_kwargs)
  ```

#### Step C: Process Response
- Extract `response.choices[0].message`
- Extract tool calls and content
- Add assistant message to conversation history

#### Step D: Execute Tool Calls
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/run_agent.py` line 3373
- Method: `self._invoke_tool(function_name, function_args, effective_task_id)`
- Returns tool result as JSON string

#### Step E: Add Tool Results to History
- Append `{"role": "tool", "tool_call_id": "...", "content": "..."}`
- Loop back to Step A with the updated message history

---

### 4. EXISTING MECHANISM TO CHANGE MODEL MID-CONVERSATION

**Yes - The `switch_model` Tool Already Exists!**

**Tool Implementation:**
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/tools/switch_model_tool.py`
- Registration: Automatic via tool discovery

**How It Works:**

1. **User calls `switch_model` tool with `action="switch"`**

2. **Tool gets routed in the agent loop:**
   - File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/run_agent.py` line 3269
   - Method: `self._invoke_tool()` detects `function_name == "switch_model"`
   - Calls: `switch_model_switch()` function

3. **switch_model_switch() calls agent method:**
   - Line 3280: `return switch_model_switch(provider=..., model=..., agent=self)`
   - Passes the agent instance to the tool

4. **Tool calls `agent._switch_model(provider, model)`:**
   - File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/run_agent.py` line 2670
   - Method: `self._switch_model(provider, model)`

5. **_switch_model() updates agent state in-place:**
   ```python
   # Resolve new provider credentials (handles auth for ANY provider)
   creds = resolve_provider_credentials(provider, model)
   
   # Apply credentials immediately - mutates agent state
   self._apply_provider_credentials(creds)
   
   # Returns success/failure dict that LLM sees as tool result
   ```

6. **_apply_provider_credentials() mutates these attributes:**
   ```python
   self.model = creds["model"]              # Update model ID
   self.provider = creds["provider"]        # Update provider
   self.base_url = creds["base_url"]        # Update endpoint
   self.api_mode = creds["api_mode"]        # Update API mode
   self.client = creds["client"]            # Replace OpenAI client instance
   self._anthropic_client = ...             # Or replace Anthropic client
   self._client_kwargs = {...}              # Store for client reconstruction
   self._use_prompt_caching = ...           # Re-evaluate feature support
   ```

7. **Next API call automatically uses the new model:**
   - The main loop calls `self._build_api_kwargs()` at line 4268
   - Which uses `self.model` (now updated)
   - And calls `self.client.chat.completions.create()` with the new client

**Key Design Point:** The tool is handled specially by the agent loop:
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/run_agent.py` line 3313
- `switch_model` is forced to execute **sequentially** (not in parallel with other tools)
- This prevents race conditions when swapping clients

---

### 5. TOOL RESULT FORMAT AND RETURN

**Tool Results Flow:**

**A. During Execution:**
- Each tool returns a **JSON string**
- Example `switch_model` success result (line 2703-2707):
  ```json
  {
    "success": true,
    "previous": {"provider": "openrouter", "model": "anthropic/claude-opus-4"},
    "current": {"provider": "openrouter", "model": "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5"},
    "message": "Model switched successfully"
  }
  ```

**B. How Results Return to LLM:**
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/run_agent.py` line 438-444 (agent_loop.py)
  ```python
  messages.append({
      "role": "tool",
      "tool_call_id": tc_id,
      "content": tool_result,  # JSON string
  })
  ```

**C. Standard Tool Result Format:**
- **Success**: `{"success": true, "result": "...", "data": {...}}`
- **Error**: `{"error": "...", "exit_code": -1, "message": "..."}`
- **Special (switch_model)**: `{"success": boolean, "current": {...}, "message": "..."}`

**D. LLM Receives Results:**
- In next API call, the tool result message is part of `messages` list
- LLM sees it as context for its next response
- Can acknowledge success, request retry, or adapt behavior

---

### 6. COULD A TOOL MODIFY THE MODEL VIA SHARED STATE MUTATION?

**Yes, Absolutely - And It's Already Happening**

**The `switch_model` Tool Already Does Exactly This:**

**Mutation Mechanism:**
1. **Agent instance is passed to tool** (line 3284)
   ```python
   agent=self,  # Pass the AIAgent instance
   ```

2. **Tool directly mutates agent attributes** (line 2693)
   ```python
   self._apply_provider_credentials(creds)  # Mutates self.model, self.client, etc.
   ```

3. **Mutations persist across API calls** because:
   - The same AIAgent instance is used throughout the conversation
   - Attributes are modified in-place
   - Next API call at line 4268 uses the updated `self.model`

**Example of Successful Mid-Conversation Model Switch:**

```
Turn 1: Agent calls with model=claude-opus (runs)
Turn 2: Agent calls switch_model tool
        → Tool calls agent._switch_model("openrouter", "openai/gpt-5.4")
        → Mutates: agent.model, agent.client, agent.provider
Turn 3: Agent calls with model=gpt-5.4 (uses new client!)
Turn 4+: All subsequent calls use gpt-5.4
```

**Safety Mechanisms in Place:**
1. **Sequential Execution** (line 3313): `switch_model` is forced sequential to prevent race conditions
2. **State Validation** (line 2683-2691): Tool validates provider is configured before switching
3. **Error Handling** (line 2709-2715): Returns detailed error if switch fails
4. **Prompt Caching Re-eval** (line 2611-2617): Updates feature support after model switch

**Other Stateful Tools That Modify Agent:**
- `delegate_task`: Passes `parent_agent=self` (line 3267) to spawn child agents
- `memory_tool`: Uses `self._memory_store` to persist user observations (line 3247-3248)
- `todo_tool`: Uses `self._todo_store` (line 3223)

---

### 7. ARCHITECTURAL STRENGTHS FOR TOOL-BASED MODEL SWITCHING

**Why This Design Works Well:**

1. **Single Source of Truth**
   - One AIAgent instance, one set of attributes
   - No client duplication or synchronization issues

2. **Atomic Transitions**
   - `_apply_provider_credentials()` updates all related state together
   - Model, base_url, api_mode, both clients, caching settings in one call

3. **Seamless Integration**
   - Next API call automatically uses new model
   - No special logic needed to "activate" the switch
   - Tool loop continues uninterrupted

4. **Provider Agnostic**
   - `resolve_provider_credentials()` works for ANY provider:
     - OpenRouter
     - Nous Portal
     - Anthropic native
     - OpenAI Codex OAuth
     - Z.AI / GLM
     - Kimi / Moonshot
     - MiniMax
     - Custom endpoints

5. **Robust Error Recovery**
   - Fallback activation (line 2621) uses same `_apply_provider_credentials()`
   - If primary model fails, can switch to fallback programmatically

---

### 8. KEY FILES FOR UNDERSTANDING THIS ARCHITECTURE

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `/run_agent.py` | Main AIAgent class, model attributes, _switch_model(), _apply_provider_credentials() |
| `/tools/switch_model_tool.py` | switch_model tool schema and handlers |
| `/agent/auxiliary_client.py` | resolve_provider_credentials() - universal provider resolver |
| `/agent/prompt_builder.py` | SWITCH_MODEL_GUIDANCE text injected into system prompt |
| `/hermes_cli/models.py` | Model catalogs and validation |
| `/agent/anthropic_adapter.py` | Anthropic-specific client setup |
| `/environments/agent_loop.py` | RL agent loop (uses same pattern) |

---

### 9. BEST APPROACH FOR A TOOL TO CHANGE THE MODEL

If you were writing a new tool that needs to switch models, follow this pattern:

```python
def my_smart_tool(action: str, agent, **kwargs) -> str:
    """Tool that can switch models mid-task."""
    if action == "switch_to_faster":
        # Agent instance is passed in kwargs (see line 3267 pattern)
        from agent.auxiliary_client import resolve_provider_credentials
        creds = resolve_provider_credentials("openrouter", "openrouter/gpt-4-mini")
        
        if creds is None:
            return json.dumps({
                "error": "openrouter provider not configured",
                "current_model": agent.model,
            })
        
        # Mutate agent state
        agent._apply_provider_credentials(creds)
        
        return json.dumps({
            "success": True,
            "previous_model": old_model,
            "current_model": agent.model,
        })
    # ... rest of tool logic
```

**The tool automatically:**
- Gets agent-level state via `parent_agent` parameter
- Resolves credentials from ANY provider uniformly
- Mutates agent in-place
- Returns JSON result for LLM to see
- Next API call uses new model automatically

---

This architecture is elegant because **model switching is just state mutation** that persists across the rest of the conversation. Tools are co-located with the agent, have direct access to its state, and can modify it with complete safety because the main loop doesn't cache model metadata.

```
     5→Usage:

    26→    hermes honcho peer --user NAME         # Set user peer name

    32→    hermes honcho tokens --context N       # Set session.context() token cap
    33→    hermes honcho tokens --dialectic N     # Set dialectic result char cap
    34→    hermes honcho identity                 # Show AI peer identity representation
```

```
     6→- Session storage (conversations persisted to disk)
     7→- Reset policy evaluation (when to start fresh)
     8→- Dynamic system prompt injection (agent knows its context)
     9→"""
    10→
    11→import logging
    12→import os
    13→import json
    14→import uuid
    15→from pathlib import Path
    16→from datetime import datetime, timedelta
    17→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    18→from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any
    19→
    20→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    21→
    22→from .config import (
    23→    Platform,
    24→    GatewayConfig,
    25→    SessionResetPolicy,
    26→    HomeChannel,
    27→)
    28→
    29→
    30→@dataclass
    31→class SessionSource:
    32→    """
    33→    Describes where a message originated from.
    34→    
    35→    This information is used to:
    36→    1. Route responses back to the right place
    37→    2. Inject context into the system prompt
    38→    3. Track origin for cron job delivery
    39→    """
    40→    platform: Platform
    41→    chat_id: str
    42→    chat_name: Optional[str] = None
    43→    chat_type: str = "dm"  # "dm", "group", "channel", "thread"
    44→    user_id: Optional[str] = None
    45→    user_name: Optional[str] = None
    46→    thread_id: Optional[str] = None  # For forum topics, Discord threads, etc.
    47→    chat_topic: Optional[str] = None  # Channel topic/description (Discord, Slack)
    48→    user_id_alt: Optional[str] = None  # Signal UUID (alternative to phone number)
    49→    chat_id_alt: Optional[str] = None  # Signal group internal ID
    50→    
    51→    @property
    52→    def description(self) -> str:
    53→        """Human-readable description of the source."""
    54→        if self.platform == Platform.LOCAL:
    55→            return "CLI terminal"
    56→        
    57→        parts = []
    58→        if self.chat_type == "dm":
    59→            parts.append(f"DM with {self.user_name or self.user_id or 'user'}")
    60→        elif self.chat_type == "group":
    61→            parts.append(f"group: {self.chat_name or self.chat_id}")
    62→        elif self.chat_type == "channel":
    63→            parts.append(f"channel: {self.chat_name or self.chat_id}")
    64→        else:
    65→            parts.append(self.chat_name or self.chat_id)
    66→        
    67→        if self.thread_id:
    68→            parts.append(f"thread: {self.thread_id}")
    69→        
    70→        return ", ".join(parts)
    71→    
    72→    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    73→        d = {
    74→            "platform": self.platform.value,
    75→            "chat_id": self.chat_id,
    76→            "chat_name": self.chat_name,
    77→            "chat_type": self.chat_type,
    78→            "user_id": self.user_id,
    79→            "user_name": self.user_name,
    80→            "thread_id": self.thread_id,
    81→            "chat_topic": self.chat_topic,
    82→        }
    83→        if self.user_id_alt:
    84→            d["user_id_alt"] = self.user_id_alt
    85→        if self.chat_id_alt:
    86→            d["chat_id_alt"] = self.chat_id_alt
    87→        return d
    88→    
    89→    @classmethod
    90→    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> "SessionSource":
    91→        return cls(
    92→            platform=Platform(data["platform"]),
    93→            chat_id=str(data["chat_id"]),
    94→            chat_name=data.get("chat_name"),
    95→            chat_type=data.get("chat_type", "dm"),
    96→            user_id=data.get("user_id"),
    97→            user_name=data.get("user_name"),
    98→            thread_id=data.get("thread_id"),
    99→            chat_topic=data.get("chat_topic"),
   100→            user_id_alt=data.get("user_id_alt"),
```

```
1→"""
     2→Gateway subcommand for hermes CLI.

     4→Handles: hermes gateway [run|start|stop|restart|status|install|uninstall|setup]
     5→"""
     6→

    23→
    24→# =============================================================================
    25→# Process Management (for manual gateway runs)
    26→# =============================================================================
    27→
    28→def find_gateway_pids() -> list:
    29→    """Find PIDs of running gateway processes."""
    30→    pids = []
    31→    patterns = [
    32→        "hermes_cli.main gateway",
    33→        "hermes gateway",
    34→        "gateway/run.py",
    35→    ]
    36→
    37→    try:
    38→        if is_windows():
    39→            # Windows: use wmic to search command lines
    40→            result = subprocess.run(
    41→                ["wmic", "process", "get", "ProcessId,CommandLine", "/FORMAT:LIST"],
    42→                capture_output=True, text=True
    43→            )
    44→            # Parse WMIC LIST output: blocks of "CommandLine=...\nProcessId=...\n"
    45→            current_cmd = ""
    46→            for line in result.stdout.split('\n'):
    47→                line = line.strip()
    48→                if line.startswith("CommandLine="):
    49→                    current_cmd = line[len("CommandLine="):]
    50→                elif line.startswith("ProcessId="):
    51→                    pid_str = line[len("ProcessId="):]
    52→                    if any(p in current_cmd for p in patterns):
    53→                        try:
    54→                            pid = int(pid_str)
    55→                            if pid != os.getpid() and pid not in pids:
    56→                                pids.append(pid)
    57→                        except ValueError:
    58→                            pass
    59→                    current_cmd = ""
    60→        else:
    61→            result = subprocess.run(
    62→                ["ps", "aux"],
    63→                capture_output=True,
    64→                text=True
    65→            )
    66→            for line in result.stdout.split('\n'):
    67→                # Skip grep and current process
    68→                if 'grep' in line or str(os.getpid()) in line:
    69→                    continue
    70→                for pattern in patterns:
    71→                    if pattern in line:
    72→                        parts = line.split()
    73→                        if len(parts) > 1:
    74→                            try:
    75→                                pid = int(parts[1])
    76→                                if pid not in pids:
    77→                                    pids.append(pid)
    78→                            except ValueError:
    79→                                continue
    80→                        break
    81→    except Exception:
    82→        pass
    83→
    84→    return pids
    85→
    86→
    87→def kill_gateway_processes(force: bool = False) -> int:
    88→    """Kill any running gateway processes. Returns count killed."""
    89→    pids = find_gateway_pids()
    90→    killed = 0
    91→    
    92→    for pid in pids:
    93→        try:
    94→            if force and not is_windows():
    95→                os.kill(pid, signal.SIGKILL)
    96→            else:
    97→                os.kill(pid, signal.SIGTERM)
    98→            killed += 1
    99→        except ProcessLookupError:
   100→            # Process already gone
   101→            pass
   102→        except PermissionError:
   103→            print(f"⚠ Permission denied to kill PID {pid}")
   104→    
   105→    return killed
   106→

   112→    return sys.platform == 'darwin'

   114→def is_windows() -> bool:
   115→    return sys.platform == 'win32'

   140→def get_hermes_cli_path() -> str:
   141→    """Get the path to the hermes CLI."""
   142→    # Check if installed via pip
   143→    import shutil
   144→    hermes_bin = shutil.which("hermes")
   145→    if hermes_bin:

   156→def generate_systemd_unit() -> str:
   157→    import shutil
   158→    python_path = get_python_path()
   159→    working_dir = str(PROJECT_ROOT)
   160→    venv_dir = str(PROJECT_ROOT / "venv")

   162→    node_bin = str(PROJECT_ROOT / "node_modules" / ".bin")
   163→
   164→    # Build a PATH that includes the venv, node_modules, and standard system dirs
   165→    sane_path = f"{venv_bin}:{node_bin}:/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin"
   166→    
   167→    hermes_cli = shutil.which("hermes") or f"{python_path} -m hermes_cli.main"
   168→    return f"""[Unit]
   169→Description={SERVICE_DESCRIPTION}
   170→After=network.target
   171→
   172→[Service]
   173→Type=simple
   174→ExecStart={python_path} -m hermes_cli.main gateway run --replace
   175→ExecStop={hermes_cli} gateway stop
   176→WorkingDirectory={working_dir}
   177→Environment="PATH={sane_path}"
   178→Environment="VIRTUAL_ENV={venv_dir}"
   179→Restart=on-failure
   180→RestartSec=10
   181→KillMode=mixed
   182→KillSignal=SIGTERM
   183→TimeoutStopSec=15
   184→StandardOutput=journal
   185→StandardError=journal
   186→
   187→[Install]
   188→WantedBy=default.target
   189→"""
   190→
   191→def systemd_install(force: bool = False):
   192→    unit_path = get_systemd_unit_path()
   193→    
   194→    if unit_path.exists() and not force:
   195→        print(f"Service already installed at: {unit_path}")
   196→        print("Use --force to reinstall")
   197→        return
   198→    
   199→    unit_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   200→    print(f"Installing systemd service to: {unit_path}")
   201→    unit_path.write_text(generate_systemd_unit())
   202→    
   203→    subprocess.run(["systemctl", "--user", "daemon-reload"], check=True)
   204→    subprocess.run(["systemctl", "--user", "enable", SERVICE_NAME], check=True)

   213→    print()

   215→    print("  sudo loginctl enable-linger $USER")
   216→
   217→def systemd_uninstall():

   225→    
   226→    subprocess.run(["systemctl", "--user", "daemon-reload"], check=True)

   390→# =============================================================================
   391→# Gateway Runner
   392→# =============================================================================
   393→
   394→def run_gateway(verbose: bool = False, replace: bool = False):
   395→    """Run the gateway in foreground.
   396→    
   397→    Args:
   398→        verbose: Enable verbose logging output.
   399→        replace: If True, kill any existing gateway instance before starting.
   400→                 This prevents systemd restart loops when the old process
   401→                 hasn't fully exited yet.
   402→    """
   403→    sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))
   404→    
   405→    from gateway.run import start_gateway
   406→    
   407→    print("┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐")
   408→    print("│           ⚕ Hermes Gateway Starting...                 │")
   409→    print("├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤")
   410→    print("│  Messaging platforms + cron scheduler                    │")
   411→    print("│  Press Ctrl+C to stop                                   │")
   412→    print("└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘")
   413→    print()
   414→    
   415→    # Exit with code 1 if gateway fails to connect any platform,
   416→    # so systemd Restart=on-failure will retry on transient errors
   417→    success = asyncio.run(start_gateway(replace=replace))
   418→    if not success:
   419→        sys.exit(1)

   486→            "   Create an App-Level Token with scope: connections:write → copy xapp-... token",

   489→            "   groups:history, im:history, im:read, im:write, users:read, files:write",

   496→            "7. Find your user ID: click your profile → three dots → Copy member ID",
   497→            "8. Invite the bot to channels: /invite @YourBot",

   499→        "vars": [

   502→            {"name": "SLACK_APP_TOKEN", "prompt": "App Token (xapp-...)", "password": True,

   505→             "is_allowlist": True,
   506→             "help": "Paste your member ID from step 7 above."},

   510→        "key": "whatsapp",
   511→        "label": "WhatsApp",
   512→        "emoji": "📲",
   513→        "token_var": "WHATSAPP_ENABLED",

   518→        "emoji": "📡",
   519→        "token_var": "SIGNAL_HTTP_URL",

   530→            "3. For other providers: use your email password or app-specific password",
   531→            "4. IMAP must be enabled on your email account",

   535→             "help": "The email address Hermes will use (e.g., [REDACTED])."},
   536→            {"name": "EMAIL_PASSWORD", "prompt": "Email password (or app password)", "password": True,
   537→             "help": "For Gmail, use an App Password (not your regular password)."},
   538→            {"name": "EMAIL_IMAP_HOST", "prompt": "IMAP host", "password": False,
   539→             "help": "e.g., imap.gmail.com for Gmail, outlook.office365.com for Outlook."},
   540→            {"name": "EMAIL_SMTP_HOST", "prompt": "SMTP host", "password": False,
   541→             "help": "e.g., smtp.gmail.com for Gmail, smtp.office365.com for Outlook."},
   542→            {"name": "EMAIL_ALLOWED_USERS", "prompt": "Allowed sender emails (comma-separated)", "password": False,

   549→
   550→def _platform_status(platform: dict) -> str:
   551→    """Return a plain-text status string for a platform.
   552→
   553→    Returns uncolored text so it can safely be embedded in
   554→    simple_term_menu items (ANSI codes break width calculation).
   555→    """
   556→    token_var = platform["token_var"]
   557→    val = get_env_value(token_var)
   558→    if token_var == "WHATSAPP_ENABLED":
   559→        if val and val.lower() == "true":
   560→            session_file = Path.home() / ".hermes" / "whatsapp" / "session" / "creds.json"
   561→            if session_file.exists():
   562→                return "configured + paired"
   563→            return "enabled, not paired"
   564→        return "not configured"
   565→    if platform.get("key") == "signal":
   566→        account = get_env_value("SIGNAL_ACCOUNT")
   567→        if val and account:
   568→            return "configured"
   569→        if val or account:
   570→            return "partially configured"
   571→        return "not configured"
   572→    if platform.get("key") == "email":
   573→        pwd = get_env_value("EMAIL_PASSWORD")
   574→        imap = get_env_value("EMAIL_IMAP_HOST")
   575→        smtp = get_env_value("EMAIL_SMTP_HOST")
   576→        if all([val, pwd, imap, smtp]):
   577→            return "configured"
   578→        if any([val, pwd, imap, smtp]):
   579→            return "partially configured"
   580→        return "not configured"
   581→    if val:
   582→        return "configured"
   583→    return "not configured"
   584→
   585→
   586→def _setup_standard_platform(platform: dict):
   587→    """Interactive setup for Telegram, Discord, or Slack."""
   588→    emoji = platform["emoji"]
   589→    label = platform["label"]
   590→    token_var = platform["token_var"]
   591→
   592→    print()
   593→    print(color(f"  ─── {emoji} {label} Setup ───", Colors.CYAN))
   594→
   595→    # Show step-by-step setup instructions if this platform has them
   596→    instructions = platform.get("setup_instructions")
   597→    if instructions:
   598→        print()
   599→        for line in instructions:
   600→            print_info(f"  {line}")
   601→
   602→    existing_token = get_env_value(token_var)
   603→    if existing_token:
   604→        print()
   605→        print_success(f"{label} is already configured.")
   606→        if not prompt_yes_no(f"  Reconfigure {label}?", False):
   607→            return
   608→
   609→    allowed_val_set = None  # Track if user set an allowlist (for home channel offer)
   610→
   611→    for var in platform["vars"]:
   612→        print()
   613→        print_info(f"  {var['help']}")
   614→        existing = get_env_value(var["name"])
   615→        if existing and var["name"] != token_var:
   616→            print_info(f"  Current: {existing}")
   617→
   618→        # Allowlist fields get special handling for the deny-by-default security model
   619→        if var.get("is_allowlist"):
   620→            print_info(f"  The gateway DENIES all users by default for security.")
   621→            print_info(f"  Enter user IDs to create an allowlist, or leave empty")
   622→            print_info(f"  and you'll be asked about open access next.")
   623→            value = prompt(f"  {var['prompt']}", password=False)
   624→            if value:
   625→                cleaned = value.replace(" ", "")
   626→                save_env_value(var["name"], cleaned)
   627→                print_success(f"  Saved — only these users can interact with the bot.")
   628→                allowed_val_set = cleaned
   629→            else:
   630→                # No allowlist — ask about open access vs DM pairing
   631→                print()
   632→                access_choices = [
   633→                    "Enable open access (anyone can message the bot)",
   634→                    "Use DM pairing (unknown users request access, you approve with 'hermes pairing approve')",
   635→                    "Skip for now (bot will deny all users until configured)",
   636→                ]
   637→                access_idx = prompt_choice("  How should unauthorized users be handled?", access_choices, 1)
   638→                if access_idx == 0:
   639→                    save_env_value("GATEWAY_ALLOW_ALL_USERS", "true")
   640→                    print_warning("  Open access enabled — anyone can use your bot!")
   641→                elif access_idx == 1:
   642→                    print_success("  DM pairing mode — users will receive a code to request access.")
   643→                    print_info("  Approve with: hermes pairing approve {platform} {code}")
   644→                else:
   645→                    print_info("  Skipped — configure later with 'hermes gateway setup'")
   646→            continue
   647→
   648→        value = prompt(f"  {var['prompt']}", [REDACTED]("password", False))
   649→        if value:
   650→            save_env_value(var["name"], value)
   651→            print_success(f"  Saved {var['name']}")
   652→        elif var["name"] == token_var:
   653→            print_warning(f"  Skipped — {label} won't work without this.")
   654→            return
   655→        else:
   656→            print_info(f"  Skipped (can configure later)")
   657→
   658→    # If an allowlist was set and home channel wasn't, offer to reuse
   659→    # the first user ID (common for Telegram DMs).
   660→    home_var = f"{label.upper()}_HOME_CHANNEL"
   661→    home_val = get_env_value(home_var)
   662→    if allowed_val_set and not home_val and label == "Telegram":
   663→        first_id = allowed_val_set.split(",")[0].strip()
   664→        if first_id and prompt_yes_no(f"  Use your user ID ({first_id}) as the home channel?", True):
   665→            save_env_value(home_var, first_id)
   666→            print_success(f"  Home channel set to {first_id}")
   667→
   668→    print()
   669→    print_success(f"{emoji} {label} configured!")
   670→
   671→
   672→def _setup_whatsapp():
   673→    """Delegate to the existing WhatsApp setup flow."""
   674→    from hermes_cli.main import cmd_whatsapp
   675→    import argparse
   676→    cmd_whatsapp(argparse.Namespace())
   677→
   678→
   679→def _is_service_installed() -> bool:
   680→    """Check if the gateway is installed as a system service."""
   681→    if is_linux():
   682→        return get_systemd_unit_path().exists()
   683→    elif is_macos():
   684→        return get_launchd_plist_path().exists()
   685→    return False
   686→
   687→
   688→def _is_service_running() -> bool:
   689→    """Check if the gateway service is currently running."""
   690→    if is_linux() and get_systemd_unit_path().exists():
   691→        result = subprocess.run(
   692→            ["systemctl", "--user", "is-active", SERVICE_NAME],
   693→            capture_output=True, text=True
   694→        )
   695→        return result.stdout.strip() == "active"
   696→    elif is_macos() and get_launchd_plist_path().exists():
   697→        result = subprocess.run(
   698→            ["launchctl", "list", "ai.hermes.gateway"],
   699→            capture_output=True, text=True
   700→        )
   701→        return result.returncode == 0
   702→    # Check for manual processes
   703→    return len(find_gateway_pids()) > 0
   704→
   705→
   706→def _setup_signal():
   707→    """Interactive setup for Signal messenger."""
   708→    import shutil
   709→
   710→    print()
   711→    print(color("  ─── 📡 Signal Setup ───", Colors.CYAN))
   712→
   713→    existing_url = get_env_value("SIGNAL_HTTP_URL")
   714→    existing_account = get_env_value("SIGNAL_ACCOUNT")
   715→    if existing_url and existing_account:
   716→        print()
   717→        print_success("Signal is already configured.")
   718→        if not prompt_yes_no("  Reconfigure Signal?", False):
   719→            return
   720→
   721→    # Check if signal-cli is available
   722→    print()
   723→    if shutil.which("signal-cli"):
   724→        print_success("signal-cli found on PATH.")
   725→    else:
   726→        print_warning("signal-cli not found on PATH.")
   727→        print_info("  Signal requires signal-cli running as an HTTP daemon.")
   728→        print_info("  Install options:")
   729→        print_info("    Linux:  sudo apt install signal-cli")
   730→        print_info("            or download from https://github.com/AsamK/signal-cli")
   731→        print_info("    macOS:  brew install signal-cli")
   732→        print_info("    Docker: bbernhard/signal-cli-rest-api")
   733→        print()
   734→        print_info("  After installing, link your account and start the daemon:")
   735→        print_info("    signal-cli link -n \"HermesAgent\"")
   736→        print_info("    signal-cli --account +YOURNUMBER daemon --http 127.0.0.1:8080")
   737→        print()
   738→
   739→    # HTTP URL
   740→    print()
   741→    print_info("  Enter the URL where signal-cli HTTP daemon is running.")
   742→    default_url = existing_url or "http://127.0.0.1:8080"
   743→    try:
   744→        url = input(f"  HTTP URL [{default_url}]: ").strip() or default_url
   745→    except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
   746→        print("\n  Setup cancelled.")
   747→        return
   748→
   749→    # Test connectivity
   750→    print_info("  Testing connection...")
   751→    try:
   752→        import httpx
   753→        resp = httpx.get(f"{url.rstrip('/')}/api/v1/check", timeout=10.0)
   754→        if resp.status_code == 200:
   755→            print_success("  signal-cli daemon is reachable!")
   756→        else:
   757→            print_warning(f"  signal-cli responded with status {resp.status_code}.")
   758→            if not prompt_yes_no("  Continue anyway?", False):
   759→                return
   760→    except Exception as e:
   761→        print_warning(f"  Could not reach signal-cli at {url}: {e}")
   762→        if not prompt_yes_no("  Save this URL anyway? (you can start signal-cli later)", True):
   763→            return
   764→
   765→    save_env_value("SIGNAL_HTTP_URL", url)
   766→
   767→    # Account phone number
   768→    print()
   769→    print_info("  Enter your Signal account phone number in E.164 format.")
   770→    print_info("  Example: +15551234567")
   771→    default_account = existing_account or ""
   772→    try:
   773→        account = input(f"  Account number{f' [{default_account}]' if default_account else ''}: ").strip()
   774→        if not account:
   775→            account = default_account
   776→    except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
   777→        print("\n  Setup cancelled.")
   778→        return
   779→
   780→    if not account:
   781→        print_error("  Account number is required.")
   782→        return
   783→
   784→    save_env_value("SIGNAL_ACCOUNT", account)
   785→
   786→    # Allowed users
   787→    print()
   788→    print_info("  The gateway DENIES all users by default for security.")
   789→    print_info("  Enter phone numbers or UUIDs of allowed users (comma-separated).")
   790→    existing_allowed = get_env_value("SIGNAL_ALLOWED_USERS") or ""
   791→    default_allowed = existing_allowed or account
   792→    try:
   793→        allowed = input(f"  Allowed users [{default_allowed}]: ").strip() or default_allowed
   794→    except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
   795→        print("\n  Setup cancelled.")
   796→        return
   797→
   798→    save_env_value("SIGNAL_ALLOWED_USERS", allowed)
   799→
   800→    # Group messaging
   801→    print()
   802→    if prompt_yes_no("  Enable group messaging? (disabled by default for security)", False):
   803→        print()
   804→        print_info("  Enter group IDs to allow, or * for all groups.")
   805→        existing_groups = get_env_value("SIGNAL_GROUP_ALLOWED_USERS") or ""
   806→        try:
   807→            groups = input(f"  Group IDs [{existing_groups or '*'}]: ").strip() or existing_groups or "*"
   808→        except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
   809→            print("\n  Setup cancelled.")
   810→            return
   811→        save_env_value("SIGNAL_GROUP_ALLOWED_USERS", groups)
   812→
   813→    print()
   814→    print_success("Signal configured!")
   815→    print_info(f"  URL: {url}")
   816→    print_info(f"  Account: {account}")
   817→    print_info(f"  DM auth: via SIGNAL_ALLOWED_USERS + DM pairing")
   818→    print_info(f"  Groups: {'enabled' if get_env_value('SIGNAL_GROUP_ALLOWED_USERS') else 'disabled'}")
   819→
   820→
   821→def gateway_setup():
   822→    """Interactive setup for messaging platforms + gateway service."""
   823→
   824→    print()
   825→    print(color("┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐", Colors.MAGENTA))
   826→    print(color("│             ⚕ Gateway Setup                            │", Colors.MAGENTA))
   827→    print(color("├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤", Colors.MAGENTA))
   828→    print(color("│  Configure messaging platforms and the gateway service. │", Colors.MAGENTA))
   829→    print(color("│  Press Ctrl+C at any time to exit.                     │", Colors.MAGENTA))
   830→    print(color("└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘", Colors.MAGENTA))
   831→
   832→    # ── Gateway service status ──
   833→    print()
   834→    service_installed = _is_service_installed()
   835→    service_running = _is_service_running()
   836→
   837→    if service_installed and service_running:
   838→        print_success("Gateway service is installed and running.")
   839→    elif service_installed:
   840→        print_warning("Gateway service is installed but not running.")
   841→        if prompt_yes_no("  Start it now?", True):
   842→            try:
   843→                if is_linux():
   844→                    systemd_start()
   845→                elif is_macos():
   846→                    launchd_start()
   847→            except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
   848→                print_error(f"  Failed to start: {e}")
   849→    else:
   850→        print_info("Gateway service is not installed yet.")
   851→        print_info("You'll be offered to install it after configuring platforms.")
   852→
   853→    # ── Platform configuration loop ──
   854→    while True:
   855→        print()
   856→        print_header("Messaging Platforms")
   857→
   858→        menu_items = []
   859→        for plat in _PLATFORMS:
   860→            status = _platform_status(plat)
   861→            menu_items.append(f"{plat['label']}  ({status})")
   862→        menu_items.append("Done")
   863→
   864→        choice = prompt_choice("Select a platform to configure:", menu_items, len(menu_items) - 1)
   865→
   866→        if choice == len(_PLATFORMS):
   867→            break
   868→
   869→        platform = _PLATFORMS[choice]
   870→
   871→        if platform["key"] == "whatsapp":
   872→            _setup_whatsapp()
   873→        elif platform["key"] == "signal":
   874→            _setup_signal()
   875→        else:
   876→            _setup_standard_platform(platform)
   877→
   878→    # ── Post-setup: offer to install/restart gateway ──
   879→    any_configured = any(
   880→        bool(get_env_value(p["token_var"]))
   881→        for p in _PLATFORMS
   882→        if p["key"] != "whatsapp"
   883→    ) or (get_env_value("WHATSAPP_ENABLED") or "").lower() == "true"
   884→
   885→    if any_configured:
   886→        print()
   887→        print(color("─" * 58, Colors.DIM))
   888→        service_installed = _is_service_installed()
   889→        service_running = _is_service_running()
   890→
   891→        if service_running:
   892→            if prompt_yes_no("  Restart the gateway to pick up changes?", True):
   893→                try:
   894→                    if is_linux():
   895→                        systemd_restart()
   896→                    elif is_macos():
   897→                        launchd_restart()
   898→                    else:
   899→                        kill_gateway_processes()
   900→                        print_info("Start manually: hermes gateway")
   901→                except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
   902→                    print_error(f"  Restart failed: {e}")
   903→        elif service_installed:
   904→            if prompt_yes_no("  Start the gateway service?", True):
   905→                try:
   906→                    if is_linux():
   907→                        systemd_start()
   908→                    elif is_macos():
   909→                        launchd_start()
   910→                except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
   911→                    print_error(f"  Start failed: {e}")
   912→        else:
   913→            print()
   914→            if is_linux() or is_macos():
   915→                platform_name = "systemd" if is_linux() else "launchd"
   916→                if prompt_yes_no(f"  Install the gateway as a {platform_name} service? (runs in background, starts on boot)", True):
   917→                    try:
   918→                        force = False
   919→                        if is_linux():
   920→                            systemd_install(force)
   921→                        else:
   922→                            launchd_install(force)
   923→                        print()
   924→                        if prompt_yes_no("  Start the service now?", True):
   925→                            try:
   926→                                if is_linux():
   927→                                    systemd_start()
   928→                                else:
   929→                                    launchd_start()
   930→                            except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
   931→                                print_error(f"  Start failed: {e}")
   932→                    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
   933→                        print_error(f"  Install failed: {e}")
   934→                        print_info("  You can try manually: hermes gateway install")
   935→                else:
   936→                    print_info("  You can install later: hermes gateway install")
   937→                    print_info("  Or run in foreground:  hermes gateway")
   938→            else:
   939→                print_info("  Service install not supported on this platform.")
   940→                print_info("  Run in foreground: hermes gateway")
   941→    else:
   942→        print()
   943→        print_info("No platforms configured. Run 'hermes gateway setup' when ready.")
   944→
   945→    print()
   946→
   947→
   948→# =============================================================================
   949→# Main Command Handler
   950→# =============================================================================
   951→
   952→def gateway_command(args):
   953→    """Handle gateway subcommands."""
   954→    subcmd = getattr(args, 'gateway_command', None)
   955→    
   956→    # Default to run if no subcommand
   957→    if subcmd is None or subcmd == "run":
   958→        verbose = getattr(args, 'verbose', False)
   959→        replace = getattr(args, 'replace', False)
   960→        run_gateway(verbose, replace=replace)
   961→        return
   962→
   963→    if subcmd == "setup":
   964→        gateway_setup()
   965→        return
   966→
   967→    # Service management commands
   968→    if subcmd == "install":
   969→        force = getattr(args, 'force', False)
   970→        if is_linux():
   971→            systemd_install(force)
   972→        elif is_macos():
   973→            launchd_install(force)
   974→        else:
   975→            print("Service installation not supported on this platform.")
   976→            print("Run manually: hermes gateway run")
   977→            sys.exit(1)
   978→    
   979→    elif subcmd == "uninstall":
   980→        if is_linux():
   981→            systemd_uninstall()
   982→        elif is_macos():
   983→            launchd_uninstall()
   984→        else:
   985→            print("Not supported on this platform.")
   986→            sys.exit(1)
   987→    
   988→    elif subcmd == "start":
   989→        if is_linux():
   990→            systemd_start()
   991→        elif is_macos():
   992→            launchd_start()
   993→        else:
   994→            print("Not supported on this platform.")
   995→            sys.exit(1)
   996→    
   997→    elif subcmd == "stop":
   998→        # Try service first, fall back to killing processes directly
   999→        service_available = False
  1000→        
  1001→        if is_linux() and get_systemd_unit_path().exists():
  1002→            try:
  1003→                systemd_stop()
  1004→                service_available = True
  1005→            except subprocess.CalledProcessError:
  1006→                pass  # Fall through to process kill
  1007→        elif is_macos() and get_launchd_plist_path().exists():
  1008→            try:
  1009→                launchd_stop()
  1010→                service_available = True
  1011→            except subprocess.CalledProcessError:
  1012→                pass
  1013→        
  1014→        if not service_available:
  1015→            # Kill gateway processes directly
  1016→            killed = kill_gateway_processes()
  1017→            if killed:
  1018→                print(f"✓ Stopped {killed} gateway process(es)")
  1019→            else:
  1020→                print("✗ No gateway processes found")
  1021→    
  1022→    elif subcmd == "restart":
  1023→        # Try service first, fall back to killing and restarting
  1024→        service_available = False
  1025→        
  1026→        if is_linux() and get_systemd_unit_path().exists():
  1027→            try:
  1028→                systemd_restart()
  1029→                service_available = True
  1030→            except subprocess.CalledProcessError:
  1031→                pass
  1032→        elif is_macos() and get_launchd_plist_path().exists():
  1033→            try:
  1034→                launchd_restart()
  1035→                service_available = True
  1036→            except subprocess.CalledProcessError:
  1037→                pass
  1038→        
  1039→        if not service_available:
  1040→            # Manual restart: kill existing processes
  1041→            killed = kill_gateway_processes()
  1042→            if killed:
  1043→                print(f"✓ Stopped {killed} gateway process(es)")
  1044→            
  1045→            import time
  1046→            time.sleep(2)
  1047→            
  1048→            # Start fresh
  1049→            print("Starting gateway...")
  1050→            run_gateway(verbose=False)
  1051→    
  1052→    elif subcmd == "status":
  1053→        deep = getattr(args, 'deep', False)
  1054→        
  1055→        # Check for service first
  1056→        if is_linux() and get_systemd_unit_path().exists():
  1057→            systemd_status(deep)
  1058→        elif is_macos() and get_launchd_plist_path().exists():
  1059→            launchd_status(deep)
  1060→        else:
  1061→            # Check for manually running processes
  1062→            pids = find_gateway_pids()
  1063→            if pids:
  1064→                print(f"✓ Gateway is running (PID: {', '.join(map(str, pids))})")
  1065→                print("  (Running manually, not as a system service)")
  1066→                print()
  1067→                print("To install as a service:")
  1068→                print("  hermes gateway install")
  1069→            else:
  1070→                print("✗ Gateway is not running")
  1071→                print()
  1072→                print("To start:")
  1073→                print("  hermes gateway          # Run in foreground")
  1074→                print("  hermes gateway install  # Install as service")
```

```
3687→async def start_gateway(config: Optional[GatewayConfig] = None, replace: bool = False) -> bool:
  3688→    """
  3689→    Start the gateway and run until interrupted.
  3690→    
  3691→    This is the main entry point for running the gateway.
  3692→    Returns True if the gateway ran successfully, False if it failed to start.
  3693→    A False return causes a non-zero exit code so systemd can auto-restart.
  3694→    
  3695→    Args:
  3696→        config: Optional gateway configuration override.
  3697→        replace: If True, kill any existing gateway instance before starting.
  3698→                 Useful for systemd services to avoid restart-loop deadlocks
  3699→                 when the previous process hasn't fully exited yet.
  3700→    """
  3701→    # ── Duplicate-instance guard ──────────────────────────────────────
  3702→    # Prevent two gateways from running under the same HERMES_HOME.
  3703→    # The PID file is scoped to HERMES_HOME, so future multi-profile
  3704→    # setups (each profile using a distinct HERMES_HOME) will naturally
  3705→    # allow concurrent instances without tripping this guard.
  3706→    import time as _time
  3707→    from gateway.status import get_running_pid, remove_pid_file
  3708→    existing_pid = get_running_pid()
  3709→    if existing_pid is not None and existing_pid != os.getpid():
  3710→        if replace:
  3711→            logger.info(
  3712→                "Replacing existing gateway instance (PID %d) with --replace.",
  3713→                existing_pid,
  3714→            )
  3715→            try:
  3716→                os.kill(existing_pid, signal.SIGTERM)
  3717→            except ProcessLookupError:
  3718→                pass  # Already gone
  3719→            except PermissionError:
  3720→                logger.error(
  3721→                    "Permission denied killing PID %d. Cannot replace.",
  3722→                    existing_pid,
  3723→                )
  3724→                return False
  3725→            # Wait up to 10 seconds for the old process to exit
  3726→            for _ in range(20):
  3727→                try:
  3728→                    os.kill(existing_pid, 0)
  3729→                    _time.sleep(0.5)
  3730→                except (ProcessLookupError, PermissionError):
  3731→                    break  # Process is gone
  3732→            else:
  3733→                # Still alive after 10s — force kill
  3734→                logger.warning(
  3735→                    "Old gateway (PID %d) did not exit after SIGTERM, sending SIGKILL.",
  3736→                    existing_pid,
  3737→                )
  3738→                try:
  3739→                    os.kill(existing_pid, signal.SIGKILL)
  3740→                    _time.sleep(0.5)
  3741→                except (ProcessLookupError, PermissionError):
  3742→                    pass
  3743→            remove_pid_file()
  3744→        else:
  3745→            hermes_home = os.getenv("HERMES_HOME", "~/.hermes")
  3746→            logger.error(
  3747→                "Another gateway instance is already running (PID %d, HERMES_HOME=%s). "
  3748→                "Use 'hermes gateway restart' to replace it, or 'hermes gateway stop' first.",
  3749→                existing_pid, hermes_home,
  3750→            )
  3751→            print(
  3752→                f"\n❌ Gateway already running (PID {existing_pid}).\n"
  3753→                f"   Use 'hermes gateway restart' to replace it,\n"
  3754→                f"   or 'hermes gateway stop' to kill it first.\n"
  3755→                f"   Or use 'hermes gateway run --replace' to auto-replace.\n"
  3756→            )
  3757→            return False
  3758→
  3759→    # Sync bundled skills on gateway start (fast -- skips unchanged)
  3760→    try:
  3761→        from tools.skills_sync import sync_skills
  3762→        sync_skills(quiet=True)
  3763→    except Exception:
  3764→        pass
  3765→
  3766→    # Configure rotating file log so gateway output is persisted for debugging
  3767→    log_dir = _hermes_home / 'logs'
  3768→    log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  3769→    file_handler = RotatingFileHandler(
  3770→        log_dir / 'gateway.log',
  3771→        maxBytes=5 * 1024 * 1024,
  3772→        backupCount=3,
  3773→    )
  3774→    from agent.redact import RedactingFormatter
  3775→    file_handler.setFormatter(RedactingFormatter('%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s: %(message)s'))
  3776→    logging.getLogger().addHandler(file_handler)
  3777→    logging.getLogger().setLevel(logging.INFO)
  3778→
  3779→    # Separate errors-only log for easy debugging
  3780→    error_handler = RotatingFileHandler(
  3781→        log_dir / 'errors.log',
  3782→        maxBytes=2 * 1024 * 1024,
  3783→        backupCount=2,
  3784→    )
  3785→    error_handler.setLevel(logging.WARNING)
  3786→    error_handler.setFormatter(RedactingFormatter('%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s: %(message)s'))
```

```
867→    async def _handle_message(self, event: MessageEvent) -> Optional[str]:
   868→        """
   869→        Handle an incoming message from any platform.
   870→        
   871→        This is the core message processing pipeline:
   872→        1. Check user authorization
   873→        2. Check for commands (/new, /reset, etc.)
   874→        3. Check for running agent and interrupt if needed
   875→        4. Get or create session
   876→        5. Build context for agent
   877→        6. Run agent conversation
   878→        7. Return response
   879→        """
   880→        source = event.source
   881→        
   882→        # Check if user is authorized
   883→        if not self._is_user_authorized(source):
   884→            logger.warning("Unauthorized user: %s (%s) on %s", source.user_id, source.user_name, source.platform.value)
   885→            # In DMs: offer pairing code. In groups: silently ignore.
   886→            if source.chat_type == "dm":
   887→                platform_name = source.platform.value if source.platform else "unknown"
   888→                code = self.pairing_store.generate_code(
   889→                    platform_name, source.user_id, source.user_name or ""
   890→                )
   891→                if code:
   892→                    adapter = self.adapters.get(source.platform)
   893→                    if adapter:
   894→                        await adapter.send(
   895→                            source.chat_id,
   896→                            f"Hi~ I don't recognize you yet!\n\n"
   897→                            f"Here's your pairing code: `{code}`\n\n"
   898→                            f"Ask the bot owner to run:\n"
   899→                            f"`hermes pairing approve {platform_name} {code}`"
   900→                        )
   901→                else:
   902→                    adapter = self.adapters.get(source.platform)
   903→                    if adapter:
   904→                        await adapter.send(
   905→                            source.chat_id,
   906→                            "Too many pairing requests right now~ "
   907→                            "Please try again later!"
   908→                        )
   909→            return None
   910→        
   911→        # PRIORITY: If an agent is already running for this session, interrupt it
   912→        # immediately. This is before command parsing to minimize latency -- the
   913→        # user's "stop" message reaches the agent as fast as possible.
   914→        [REDACTED](source)
   915→        if _quick_key in self._running_agents:
   916→            running_agent = self._running_agents[_quick_key]
   917→            logger.debug("PRIORITY interrupt for session %s", _quick_key[:20])
   918→            running_agent.interrupt(event.text)
   919→            if _quick_key in self._pending_messages:
   920→                self._pending_messages[_quick_key] += "\n" + event.text
   921→            else:
   922→                self._pending_messages[_quick_key] = event.text
   923→            return None
   924→        
   925→        # Check for commands
   926→        command = event.get_command()
   927→        
   928→        # Emit command:* hook for any recognized slash command
   929→        _known_commands = {"new", "reset", "help", "status", "stop", "model",
   930→                          "personality", "retry", "undo", "sethome", "set-home",
   931→                          "compress", "usage", "insights", "reload-mcp", "reload_mcp",
   932→                          "update", "title", "resume", "provider", "rollback",
   933→                          "background", "reasoning"}
   934→        if command and command in _known_commands:
   935→            await self.hooks.emit(f"command:{command}", {
   936→                "platform": source.platform.value if source.platform else "",
   937→                "user_id": source.user_id,
   938→                "command": command,
   939→                "args": event.get_command_args().strip(),
   940→            })
   941→        
   942→        if command in ["new", "reset"]:
   943→            return await self._handle_reset_command(event)
   944→        
   945→        if command == "help":
   946→            return await self._handle_help_command(event)
   947→        
   948→        if command == "status":
   949→            return await self._handle_status_command(event)
   950→        
   951→        if command == "stop":
   952→            return await self._handle_stop_command(event)
   953→        
   954→        if command == "model":
   955→            return await self._handle_model_command(event)
   956→        
   957→        if command == "provider":
   958→            return await self._handle_provider_command(event)
   959→        
   960→        if command == "personality":
   961→            return await self._handle_personality_command(event)
   962→        
   963→        if command == "retry":
   964→            return await self._handle_retry_command(event)
   965→        
   966→        if command == "undo":

   968→        
   969→        if command in ["sethome", "set-home"]:
   970→            return await self._handle_set_home_command(event)
   971→
   972→        if command == "compress":

   974→
   975→        if command == "usage":

   983→

   986→

   989→
```

```
325→class SessionStore:
   326→    """
   327→    Manages session storage and retrieval.
   328→    
   329→    Uses SQLite (via SessionDB) for session metadata and message transcripts.
   330→    Falls back to legacy JSONL files if SQLite is unavailable.
   331→    """
   332→    
   333→    def __init__(self, sessions_dir: Path, config: GatewayConfig,
   334→                 has_active_processes_fn=None,
   335→                 on_auto_reset=None):
   336→        self.sessions_dir = sessions_dir
   337→        self.config = config
   338→        self._entries: Dict[str, SessionEntry] = {}
   339→        self._loaded = False
   340→        self._has_active_processes_fn = has_active_processes_fn
   341→        # on_auto_reset is deprecated — memory flush now runs proactively
   342→        # via the background session expiry watcher in GatewayRunner.

   344→

   352→    
   353→    def _ensure_loaded(self) -> None:
   354→        """Load sessions index from disk if not already loaded."""
   355→        if self._loaded:
   356→            return
   357→        
   358→        self.sessions_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   359→        sessions_file = self.sessions_dir / "sessions.json"
   360→        
   361→        if sessions_file.exists():
   362→            try:
   363→                with open(sessions_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   364→                    data = json.load(f)
   365→                    for key, entry_data in data.items():
   366→                        self._entries[key] = SessionEntry.from_dict(entry_data)
   367→            except Exception as e:
   368→                print(f"[gateway] Warning: Failed to load sessions: {e}")
   369→        
   370→        self._loaded = True

   372→    def _save(self) -> None:
   373→        """Save sessions index to disk (kept for session key -> ID mapping)."""
   374→        import tempfile
   375→        self.sessions_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   376→        sessions_file = self.sessions_dir / "sessions.json"
   377→
   378→        data = {key: entry.to_dict() for key, entry in self._entries.items()}
   379→        fd, tmp_path = tempfile.mkstemp(
   380→            dir=str(self.sessions_dir), suffix=".tmp", prefix=".sessions_"
   381→        )
   382→        try:
   383→            with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   384→                json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
   385→                f.flush()
   386→                os.fsync(f.fileno())
   387→            os.replace(tmp_path, sessions_file)
   388→        except BaseException:
   389→            try:
   390→                os.unlink(tmp_path)
   391→            except OSError as e:
   392→                logger.debug("Could not remove temp file %s: %s", tmp_path, e)
   393→            raise

   399→    def _is_session_expired(self, entry: SessionEntry) -> bool:
   400→        """Check if a session has expired based on its reset policy.
   401→        
   402→        Works from the entry alone — no SessionSource needed.
   403→        Used by the background expiry watcher to proactively flush memories.
   404→        Sessions with active background processes are never considered expired.
   405→        """
   406→        if self._has_active_processes_fn:
   407→            if self._has_active_processes_fn(entry.session_key):

   409→
   410→        policy = self.config.get_reset_policy(

   413→        )
   414→
   415→        if policy.mode == "none":
   416→            return False
   417→
   418→        now = datetime.now()
   419→
   420→        if policy.mode in ("idle", "both"):
   421→            idle_deadline = entry.updated_at + timedelta(minutes=policy.idle_minutes)
   422→            if now > idle_deadline:
   423→                return True
   424→
   425→        if policy.mode in ("daily", "both"):
   426→            today_reset = now.replace(
   427→                hour=policy.at_hour,
   428→                minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0,
   429→            )
   430→            if now.hour < policy.at_hour:
   431→                today_reset -= timedelta(days=1)
   432→            if entry.updated_at < today_reset:
   433→                return True
   434→
   435→        return False
```

```
class _SafeWriter:
    """Transparent stdout wrapper that catches OSError from broken pipes.

    When hermes-agent runs as a systemd service, Docker container, or headless
    daemon, the stdout pipe can become unavailable (idle timeout, buffer
    exhaustion, socket reset). Any print() call then raises
    ``OSError: [Errno 5] Input/output error``, which can crash
    run_conversation() — especially via double-fault when the except handler
    also tries to print.

    This wrapper delegates all writes to the underlying stream and silently
    catches OSError.  It is installed once at the start of run_conversation()
    and is transparent when stdout is healthy (zero overhead on the happy path).
    """

    __slots__ = ("_inner",)

    def __init__(self, inner):
        object.__setattr__(self, "_inner", inner)

    def write(self, data):
        try:
            return self._inner.write(data)
        except OSError:
            return len(data) if isinstance(data, str) else 0

    def flush(self):
        try:
            self._inner.flush()
        except OSError:
            pass

    def fileno(self):
        return self._inner.fileno()

    def isatty(self):
        try:
            return self._inner.isatty()
```
