```
1→"""Living-plan update helpers used by resolve command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import logging
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→from typing import NamedTuple
     9→
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.transition_messages import emit_transition_message
    11→from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
    12→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    13→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.plan_load import warn_plan_load_degraded_once
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync import live_planned_queue_empty, reconcile_plan
    16→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import (
    17→    append_log_entry,
    18→    auto_complete_steps,
    19→    purge_ids,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._plan.refresh_lifecycle import (
    22→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    23→    clear_postflight_scan_completion,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import (
    26→    add_uncommitted_issues,
    27→    has_living_plan,
    28→    load_plan,
    29→    plan_path_for_state,
    30→    purge_uncommitted_ids,
    31→    save_plan,
    32→)
    33→
    34→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    35→
    36→
    37→class ClusterContext(NamedTuple):
    38→    cluster_name: str | None
    39→    cluster_completed: bool
    40→    cluster_remaining: int
    41→
    42→
    43→def _reconcile_if_queue_drained(plan: dict, state: dict | None) -> str | None:
    44→    """Advance the living plan when a resolve drains the explicit live queue.
    45→
    46→    Returns the new lifecycle phase if a transition occurred, so the caller
    47→    can emit the directive after all other output.
    48→    """
    49→    if state is None or not live_planned_queue_empty(plan):
    50→        return None
    51→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(state.get("config"))
    52→    result = reconcile_plan(plan, state, target_strict=target_strict)
    53→    if result is not None and result.lifecycle_phase_changed:
    54→        return result.lifecycle_phase
    55→    return None
    56→
    57→
    58→def capture_cluster_context(plan: dict, resolved_ids: list[str]) -> ClusterContext:
    59→    """Determine cluster membership for resolved issues before purge."""
    60→    clusters = plan.get("clusters") or {}
    61→    overrides = plan.get("overrides") or {}
    62→    cluster_name: str | None = None
    63→    for resolved_id in resolved_ids:
    64→        override = overrides.get(resolved_id)
    65→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
    66→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    67→            break
    68→    if not cluster_name or cluster_name not in clusters:
    69→        return ClusterContext(cluster_name=None, cluster_completed=False, cluster_remaining=0)
    70→    current_ids = set(clusters[cluster_name].get("issue_ids") or [])
    71→    remaining = current_ids - set(resolved_ids)
    72→    return ClusterContext(
    73→        cluster_name=cluster_name,
    74→        cluster_completed=len(remaining) == 0,
    75→        cluster_remaining=len(remaining),
    76→    )
    77→
    78→
    79→def update_living_plan_after_resolve(
    80→    *,
    81→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    82→    all_resolved: list[str],
    83→    attestation: str | None,
    84→    state: dict | None = None,
    85→    state_file: Path | str | None = None,
    86→) -> tuple[dict | None, ClusterContext]:
    87→    """Apply resolve side effects to the living plan when it exists."""
    88→    plan_path = plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file)) if state_file else None
    89→    plan = None
    90→    ctx = ClusterContext(cluster_name=None, cluster_completed=False, cluster_remaining=0)
    91→    try:
    92→        if not has_living_plan(plan_path):
    93→            return None, ctx
    94→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
    95→        ctx = capture_cluster_context(plan, all_resolved)
    96→        purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    97→        step_messages = auto_complete_steps(plan)
    98→        for msg in step_messages:
    99→            print(colorize(msg, "green"))
   100→        append_log_entry(
   101→            plan,
   102→            "resolve",
   103→            issue_ids=all_resolved,
   104→            actor="user",
   105→            note=getattr(args, "note", None),
   106→            detail={"status": args.status, "attestation": attestation},
   107→        )
   108→        if ctx.cluster_completed and ctx.cluster_name:
   109→            append_log_entry(
   110→                plan,
   111→                "cluster_done",
   112→                issue_ids=all_resolved,
   113→                cluster_name=ctx.cluster_name,
   114→                actor="user",
   115→            )
   116→        if args.status == "fixed":
   117→            add_uncommitted_issues(plan, all_resolved)
   118→        elif args.status == "open":
   119→            purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, all_resolved)
   120→        transition_phase: str | None = None
   121→        if clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=all_resolved, state=state):
   122→            transition_phase = LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   123→        reconcile_phase = _reconcile_if_queue_drained(plan, state)
   124→        if reconcile_phase:
   125→            transition_phase = reconcile_phase
   126→        save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   127→        if purged:
   128→            print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
   129→        if transition_phase:
   130→            emit_transition_message(transition_phase)
   131→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   132→        _logger.debug("plan update failed after resolve", exc_info=True)
   133→        warn_plan_load_degraded_once(
   134→            command_label="resolve",
   135→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
   136→            behavior="Living-plan queue metadata could not be updated after resolve.",
   137→        )
   138→    return plan, ctx
   139→
   140→
   141→__all__ = ["ClusterContext", "capture_cluster_context", "update_living_plan_after_resolve"]
   142→
```

```
    55→
    56→def _validate_skip_requirements(
    57→    *,
    58→    kind: str,
    59→    attestation: str | None,
    60→    note: str | None,
    61→) -> bool:
    62→    if not skip_kind_requires_attestation(kind):
    63→        return True
    64→    if not validate_attestation(
    65→        attestation,
    66→        required_phrases=_TRIAGE_SKIP_ATTESTATION_PHRASES,
    67→    ):
    68→        show_attestation_requirement(
    69→            "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
    70→            attestation,
    71→            _TRIAGE_SKIP_ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    72→            required_phrases=_TRIAGE_SKIP_ATTESTATION_PHRASES,
    73→        )
    74→        return False
    75→    if skip_kind_requires_note(kind) and not note:
    76→        print(
    77→            colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
    78→            file=sys.stderr,
    79→        )
    80→        return False
    81→    return True
    82→
    83→
    84→def _apply_state_skip_resolution(
    85→    *,
    86→    kind: str,
    87→    state_file: Path | None,
    88→    issue_ids: list[str],
    89→    note: str | None,
    90→    attestation: str | None,
    91→) -> dict | None:
    92→    status = skip_kind_state_status(kind)
    93→    if status is None:
    94→        return None
    95→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    96→    for fid in issue_ids:
    97→        state_mod.resolve_issues(
    98→            state_data,
    99→            fid,
   100→            status,
   101→            note or "",
   102→            attestation=attestation,
   103→        )
   104→    return state_data
   105→
   106→
   107→def _validate_temporary_skip_confirmation(
   108→    *,
   109→    confirm: bool,
   110→    kind: str,
   111→    reason: str | None,
   112→    attestation: str | None,
   113→) -> bool:
   114→    if not confirm or kind != "temporary":
   115→        return True
   116→    if not reason:
   117→        print(
   118→            colorize(
   119→                "  --confirm requires --reason to describe why this is being deferred.",
   120→                "red",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→        return False
   124→    required_phrases = ("i have actually reflected", "not deferring")
   125→    normalized_attest = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
   126→    missing = [phrase for phrase in required_phrases if phrase not in normalized_attest]
   127→    if not missing:
   128→        return True
   129→    print(
   130→        colorize(
   131→            "  Deferring items requires you to confirm you've thought this through.",
   132→            "yellow",
   133→        )
   134→    )
   135→    print(colorize("  Add this to your command:", "dim"))
   136→    print()
   137→    print(
   138→        colorize(
   139→            '    --attest "I have actually reflected and '
   140→            'I am not deferring this for lazy reasons."',
   141→            "dim",
   142→        )
   143→    )
   144→    print()
   145→    return False
   146→
   147→
   148→def _warn_or_block_bulk_skip(issue_ids: list[str], *, confirm: bool) -> None:
   149→    if len(issue_ids) <= _BULK_SKIP_THRESHOLD:
   150→        return
   151→    print(
   152→        colorize(
   153→            f"  Bulk skip: {len(issue_ids)} items will be removed from the active queue.",
   154→            "yellow",

   156→        file=sys.stderr,
   157→    )
   158→    if confirm:
   159→        return
   160→    raise CommandError(
   161→        f"Skipping {len(issue_ids)} items requires --confirm. "
   162→        "Review the items first, or skip individually."
   163→    )
   164→
   165→
   166→def _save_skip_plan_state(
   167→    *,

   172→) -> None:

   174→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)

   176→    save_plan_state_transactional(
   177→        plan=plan,
   178→        plan_path=plan_file,
   179→        state_data=state_data,
   180→        state_path_value=state_file,
   181→    )

   184→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   185→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   186→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   187→    state = runtime.state
   188→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   189→        return
   190→
   191→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   192→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
   193→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
   194→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
   195→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
   196→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   197→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   198→
   199→    kind = skip_kind_from_flags(permanent=permanent, false_positive=false_positive)
   200→
   201→    if not _validate_temporary_skip_confirmation(
   202→        confirm=bool(getattr(args, "confirm", False)),
   203→        kind=kind,
   204→        reason=reason,
   205→        attestation=attestation,
   206→    ):
   207→        return
   208→    if not _validate_skip_requirements(kind=kind, attestation=attestation, note=note):
   209→        raise CommandError(
   210→            "Invalid plan skip attestation or note.",
   211→            exit_code=2,
   212→        )
   213→
   214→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   215→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   216→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   217→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   218→    if not issue_ids:
   219→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   220→        return
   221→
   222→    _warn_or_block_bulk_skip(issue_ids, confirm=bool(getattr(args, "confirm", False)))
   223→
   224→    state_data = _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   225→        kind=kind,
   226→        state_file=state_file,
   227→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   228→        note=note,
   229→        attestation=attestation,
   230→    )
   231→
   232→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   233→    count = skip_items(
   234→        plan,
   235→        issue_ids,
   236→        kind=kind,
   237→        reason=reason,
   238→        note=note,
   239→        attestation=attestation,
   240→        review_after=review_after,
   241→        scan_count=scan_count,
   242→    )
   243→
   244→    append_log_entry(
   245→        plan,
   246→        "skip",
   247→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   248→        actor="user",
   249→        note=note,
   250→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   251→    )
   252→    transition_phase: str | None = None
   253→    if clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=issue_ids, state=state):
   254→        transition_phase = LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   255→    _save_skip_plan_state(
   256→        plan=plan,
   257→        plan_file=plan_file,
   258→        state_data=state_data,
   259→        state_file=state_file,
   260→    )
   261→
   262→    print(colorize(f"  {SKIP_KIND_LABELS[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   263→    if review_after:
   264→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   265→    if kind == "temporary":
   266→        print(
   267→            colorize(
   268→                "  If you're actually not going to fix these, use "
   269→                "`desloppify plan skip <patterns> --permanent` instead to wontfix them.",
   270→                "dim",
   271→            )
   272→        )
   273→    print_user_message(
   274→        "Hey — if skipping was the right call, just continue with"
   275→        " what you were doing. If you think a broader re-triage is"
   276→        " needed, use `desloppify plan triage`. Run `desloppify"
   277→        " plan --help` to see all available plan tools. Otherwise"
   278→        " no need to reply, just keep going."
   279→    )
   280→    if transition_phase:
   281→        emit_transition_message(transition_phase)
   282→
   283→
   284→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   285→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   286→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   287→    state = runtime.state
   288→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   289→        return
   290→
   291→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   292→
   293→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   294→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   295→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   296→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   297→    if not issue_ids:
   298→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   299→        return
   300→
   301→    include_protected = bool(getattr(args, "force", False))
   302→    count, need_reopen, protected_kept = unskip_items(
   303→        plan,
   304→        issue_ids,
   305→        include_protected=include_protected,
   306→    )
   307→    unskipped_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in protected_kept]
   308→    append_log_entry(
   309→        plan,
   310→        "unskip",
   311→        issue_ids=unskipped_ids,
   312→        actor="user",
   313→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   314→    )
   315→    transition_phase: str | None = None
   316→    if clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=unskipped_ids, state=state):
   317→        transition_phase = LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   318→
   319→    reopened: list[str] = []
   320→    if need_reopen:
   321→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   322→        for fid in need_reopen:
   323→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open"))
   324→        save_plan_state_transactional(
   325→            plan=plan,
   326→            plan_path=plan_file,
   327→            state_data=state_data,
   328→            state_path_value=state_file,
   329→        )
   330→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   331→    else:
   332→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   333→
   334→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   335→    if protected_kept:
   336→        print(
   337→            colorize(
   338→                f"  Kept {len(protected_kept)} protected skip(s) "
   339→                f"(permanent/false_positive with notes). Use --force to override.",
   340→                "yellow",
   341→            )
   342→        )
   343→    if transition_phase:
   344→        emit_transition_message(transition_phase)
   345→
   346→
   347→def cmd_plan_backlog(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   348→    """Move deferred items to backlog — remove from plan tracking entirely."""
   349→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   350→    state = runtime.state
   351→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   352→        return
   353→
   354→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   355→
   356→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   357→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   358→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   359→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   360→    if not issue_ids:
   361→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   362→        return
   363→
   364→    removed = backlog_items(plan, issue_ids)
   365→    if not removed:
   366→        print(colorize("  No matching deferred items found.", "yellow"))
   367→        return
   368→
   369→    # Reopen issues whose state status was set by the skip (deferred/triaged_out)
   370→    # so they don't get stranded with a non-open status while untracked by plan.
   371→    _BACKLOG_REOPEN_STATUSES = {"deferred", "triaged_out"}
   372→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   373→    issues = state_data.get("work_items", {})
   374→    reopen_ids = [
   375→        fid for fid in removed
   376→        if issues.get(fid, {}).get("status") in _BACKLOG_REOPEN_STATUSES
   377→    ]
   378→
   379→    if reopen_ids:
   380→        for fid in reopen_ids:
   381→            state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open")
   382→
   383→    append_log_entry(
   384→        plan,
   385→        "backlog",
   386→        issue_ids=removed,
   387→        actor="user",
   388→    )
   389→    transition_phase: str | None = None
   390→    if clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=removed, state=state_data):
   391→        transition_phase = LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   392→
   393→    if reopen_ids:
   394→        save_plan_state_transactional(
   395→            plan=plan,
   396→            plan_path=plan_file,
   397→            state_data=state_data,
   398→            state_path_value=state_file,
   399→        )
   400→    else:
   401→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   402→
   403→    print(colorize(f"  Moved {len(removed)} item(s) to backlog.", "green"))
   404→    if reopen_ids:
   405→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopen_ids)} deferred/triaged-out issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   406→    if transition_phase:
   407→        emit_transition_message(transition_phase)
   408→
   409→
   410→__all__ = [
   411→    "_apply_state_skip_resolution",
   412→    "_validate_skip_requirements",
   413→    "cmd_plan_backlog",
   414→    "cmd_plan_skip",
   415→    "cmd_plan_unskip",
   416→]
```

```
    14→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT,
    15→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL,
    16→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT,
    17→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    18→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT,
    19→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT,
    20→    current_lifecycle_phase,
    21→    set_lifecycle_phase,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.dimensions import sync_subjective_dimensions
    24→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.phase_cleanup import prune_synthetic_for_phase
    25→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.triage import sync_triage_needed
    26→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.snapshot import build_triage_snapshot
    27→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.workflow import (
    28→    ScoreSnapshot,
    29→    _subjective_review_current_for_cycle,
    30→    sync_communicate_score_needed,
    31→    sync_create_plan_needed,
    32→)
    33→
    34→
    35→@dataclass
    36→class ReconcileResult:
    37→    """Mutation summary for one boundary-triggered reconcile pass."""
    38→
    39→    subjective: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    40→    auto_cluster_changes: int = 0
    41→    communicate_score: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    42→    create_plan: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    43→    triage: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    44→    lifecycle_phase: str = ""
    45→    lifecycle_phase_changed: bool = False
    46→    phase_cleanup_pruned: list[str] | None = None
    47→
    48→    @property
    49→    def dirty(self) -> bool:
    50→        return any(
    51→            (
    52→                self.subjective is not None and bool(self.subjective.changes),
    53→                self.auto_cluster_changes > 0,
    54→                self.communicate_score is not None
    55→                and bool(self.communicate_score.changes),
    56→                self.create_plan is not None
    57→                and bool(self.create_plan.changes),
    58→                self.triage is not None
    59→                and bool(
    60→                    self.triage.changes
    61→                    or getattr(self.triage, "deferred", False)
    62→                ),
    63→                self.lifecycle_phase_changed,
    64→                bool(self.phase_cleanup_pruned),
    65→            )
    66→        )
    67→
    68→    @property
    69→    def workflow_injected_ids(self) -> list[str]:
    70→        injected: list[str] = []
    71→        for result in (self.communicate_score, self.create_plan):
    72→            if result is None:
    73→                continue
    74→            injected.extend(list(result.injected))
    75→        return injected
    76→
    77→
    78→def _current_scores(state: dict) -> ScoreSnapshot:
    79→    snapshot = score_snapshot(state)
    80→    return ScoreSnapshot(
    81→        strict=snapshot.strict,
    82→        overall=snapshot.overall,
    83→        objective=snapshot.objective,
    84→        verified=snapshot.verified,
    85→    )
    86→
    87→
    88→def _log_gate_changes(plan: dict, action: str, detail: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    89→    append_log_entry(plan, action, actor="system", detail=detail)
    90→
    91→
    92→def _resolve_reconcile_phase(
    93→    plan: dict,
    94→    state: dict,
    95→    *,
    96→    result: ReconcileResult,
    97→    policy: object | None,
    98→) -> str:
    99→    order = [item for item in plan.get("queue_order", []) if isinstance(item, str)]
   100→    if result.workflow_injected_ids or any(item.startswith("workflow::") for item in order):
   101→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   102→
   103→    if result.triage and (result.triage.injected or result.triage.deferred):
   104→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   105→    if any(item.startswith("triage::") for item in order):
   106→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   107→
   108→    subjective_ids = [item for item in order if item.startswith("subjective::")]
   109→    if subjective_ids:
   110→        unscored_ids = set(getattr(policy, "unscored_ids", ()) or ())
   111→        if any(item in unscored_ids for item in subjective_ids):
   112→            return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL
   113→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT
   114→
   115→    triage_snapshot = build_triage_snapshot(plan, state)
   116→    if (
   117→        triage_snapshot.triage_has_run
   118→        and not triage_snapshot.has_triage_in_queue
   119→        and not triage_snapshot.is_triage_stale
   120→        and bool(triage_snapshot.live_open_ids)
   121→    ):
   122→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   123→
   124→    persisted = current_lifecycle_phase(plan)
   125→    if persisted:
   126→        return persisted
   127→    if open_review_ids(state):
   128→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   129→    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   130→
   131→
   132→def live_planned_queue_empty(plan: dict) -> bool:
   133→    """Return True when queue_order has no remaining substantive items.
   134→
   135→    Overrides and clusters are ownership metadata — they must never expand
   136→    the live queue.  Only explicit ``queue_order`` entries count.
   137→    """
   138→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   139→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   140→    return not any(
   141→        isinstance(item_id, str)
   142→        and item_id not in skipped
   143→        and not is_synthetic_id(item_id)
   144→        for item_id in order
   145→    )
   146→
   147→
   148→def reconcile_plan(plan: dict, state: dict, *, target_strict: float) -> ReconcileResult:
   149→    """Run the shared boundary reconciliation pipeline."""
   150→    result = ReconcileResult()
   151→    if not live_planned_queue_empty(plan):
   152→        return result
   153→
   154→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   155→        state,
   156→        target_strict=target_strict,
   157→        plan=plan,
   158→    )
   159→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   160→
   161→    # Skip subjective sync when workflow will supersede it: all dims are
   162→    # scored and communicate-score hasn't fired yet this cycle.  The phase
   163→    # cleanup safety net still prunes if this peek is wrong.
   164→    will_inject_workflow = (
   165→        "previous_plan_start_scores" not in plan
   166→        and _subjective_review_current_for_cycle(
   167→            plan,
   168→            state,
   169→            policy=policy,
   170→        )
   171→    )
   172→    if will_inject_workflow:
   173→        result.subjective = QueueSyncResult()
   174→    else:
   175→        result.subjective = sync_subjective_dimensions(
   176→            plan,
   177→            state,
   178→            policy=policy,
   179→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   180→        )
   181→        if result.subjective.changes:
   182→            _log_gate_changes(plan, "sync_subjective", {"changes": True})
   183→
   184→    if will_inject_workflow:
   185→        result.auto_cluster_changes = 0
   186→    else:
   187→        result.auto_cluster_changes = int(
   188→            auto_cluster_issues(
   189→                plan,
   190→                state,
   191→                target_strict=target_strict,
   192→                policy=policy,
   193→            )
   194→        )
   195→        if result.auto_cluster_changes:
   196→            _log_gate_changes(plan, "auto_cluster", {"changes": True})
   197→
   198→    result.communicate_score = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   199→        plan,
   200→        state,
   201→        policy=policy,
   202→        current_scores=_current_scores(state),
   203→    )
   204→    if result.communicate_score.changes:
   205→        _log_gate_changes(plan, "sync_communicate_score", {"injected": True})
   206→
   207→    result.create_plan = sync_create_plan_needed(
   208→        plan,
   209→        state,
   210→        policy=policy,
   211→    )
   212→    if result.create_plan.changes:
   213→        _log_gate_changes(plan, "sync_create_plan", {"injected": True})
   214→
   215→    result.triage = sync_triage_needed(
   216→        plan,
   217→        state,
   218→        policy=policy,
   219→    )
   220→    if result.triage.injected:
   221→        _log_gate_changes(plan, "sync_triage", {"injected": True})
   222→
   223→    result.lifecycle_phase = _resolve_reconcile_phase(
   224→        plan,
   225→        state,
   226→        result=result,
   227→        policy=policy,
   228→    )
   229→    result.lifecycle_phase_changed = set_lifecycle_phase(plan, result.lifecycle_phase)
   230→    if result.lifecycle_phase_changed:
   231→        _log_gate_changes(
   232→            plan,
   233→            "sync_lifecycle_phase",
   234→            {"phase": result.lifecycle_phase},
   235→        )
   236→
   237→    result.phase_cleanup_pruned = prune_synthetic_for_phase(plan, result.lifecycle_phase)
   238→    if result.phase_cleanup_pruned:
   239→        _log_gate_changes(
   240→            plan,
   241→            "phase_transition_cleanup",
   242→            {"phase": result.lifecycle_phase, "pruned": list(result.phase_cleanup_pruned)},
   243→        )
   244→
   245→    return result
   246→
   247→
   248→__all__ = ["ReconcileResult", "live_planned_queue_empty", "reconcile_plan"]
   249→
```

```
    36→
    37→def _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    38→    """Clear all cycle state when --force-rescan is used."""
    39→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    40→    synthetic = [item for item in order if is_synthetic_id(item)]
    41→    if not synthetic and not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    42→        return False
    43→    for item in synthetic:
    44→        order.remove(item)
    45→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    46→    clear_score_communicated_sentinel(plan)
    47→    plan.pop("scan_count_at_plan_start", None)
    48→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    49→    if isinstance(meta, dict):
    50→        meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
    51→    count = len(synthetic)
    52→    if count:
    53→        print(
    54→            colorize(
    55→                f"  Plan: force-rescan — removed {count} synthetic item(s) "
    56→                f"and reset cycle state.",
    57→                "yellow",
    58→            )
    59→        )
    60→    return True
    61→
    62→
    63→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    64→    return bool(
    65→        plan.get("queue_order")
    66→        or plan.get("overrides")
    67→        or plan.get("clusters")
    68→        or plan.get("skipped")
    69→    )
    70→
    71→
    72→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
    73→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    74→        return False
    75→    recon = reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, state)
    76→    if recon.resurfaced:
    77→        print(
    78→            colorize(
    79→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    80→                "cyan",
    81→            )
    82→        )
    83→    return bool(recon.changes)
    84→
    85→
    86→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
    87→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
    88→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    89→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
    90→        return False
    91→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
    92→        return False
    93→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    94→    if scores.strict is None:
    95→        return False
    96→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
    97→        "strict": scores.strict,
    98→        "overall": scores.overall,
    99→        "objective": scores.objective,
   100→        "verified": scores.verified,
   101→    }
   102→    clear_score_communicated_sentinel(plan)
   103→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   104→    return True
   105→
   106→
   107→def _has_objective_cycle(
   108→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   109→    plan: dict[str, object],
   110→) -> bool | None:
   111→    """Return True when objective queue work exists and a cycle baseline should freeze."""
   112→    try:
   113→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_breakdown
   114→
   115→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   116→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   117→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "compute queue breakdown for plan-start seeding", exc)
   118→        return None
   119→    return breakdown.objective_actionable > 0
   120→
   121→
   122→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   123→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   124→) -> bool:
   125→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   126→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   127→        return False
   128→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   129→        return False
   130→
   131→    try:
   132→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   133→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   134→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   135→            score_display_mode,
   136→        )
   137→
   138→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   139→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   140→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   141→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   142→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   143→        return False
   144→    if not queue_empty:
   145→        return False
   146→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   147→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   148→    clear_score_communicated_sentinel(plan)
   149→    return True
   150→

   152→def _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(
   153→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   154→    plan: dict[str, object],
   155→) -> bool:
   156→    """Record that the cycle's post-review scan has completed."""
   157→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores") and current_unscored_ids(state):
   158→        return False
   159→    if build_deferred_disposition_item(plan) is not None:
   160→        return False
   161→    return mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   162→        plan,

   164→    )

   205→def _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   206→    """Return True when a plan cycle is active and queue items remain."""

   212→def _display_reconcile_results(
   213→    result: ReconcileResult,
   214→    plan: dict,
   215→    *,
   216→    mid_cycle: bool,
   217→) -> None:
   218→    subjective = result.subjective
   219→    if subjective and subjective.resurfaced:
   220→        print(

   223→                "yellow",

   225→        )
   226→    if subjective and subjective.pruned:
   227→        print(
   228→            colorize(

   230→                "cyan",
   231→            )
   232→        )
   233→    if subjective and subjective.injected:
   234→        print(
   235→            colorize(
   236→                f"  Plan: {len(subjective.injected)} subjective dimension(s) queued for review.",
   237→                "cyan",
   238→            )
   239→        )
   240→    if mid_cycle and not result.auto_cluster_changes:
   241→        print(
   242→            colorize(
   243→                "  Plan: mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration to preserve queue state.",
   244→                "dim",
   245→            )
   246→        )
   247→    if result.create_plan and result.create_plan.injected:
   248→        print(
   249→            colorize(
   250→                "  Plan: reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued.",
   251→                "cyan",
   252→            )
   253→        )
   254→    if (
   255→        result.triage
   256→        and result.triage.deferred
   257→        and plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_recommended")
   258→    ):
   259→        print(
   260→            colorize(
   261→                "  Plan: review work items changed — triage recommended after current work.",
   262→                "dim",
   263→            )
   264→        )
   265→    if result.triage and result.triage.injected:
   266→        print(
   267→            colorize(
   268→                "  Plan: planning mode needed — review work items changed since last triage.",
   269→                "cyan",
   270→            )
   271→        )
   272→
   273→
   274→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
   275→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   276→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   277→    try:
   278→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   279→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   280→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   281→        return
   282→
   283→    force_rescan = getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False)
   284→    dirty = _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan) if force_rescan else False
   285→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_without_policy(plan=plan, state=runtime.state) or dirty
   286→
   287→    boundary_crossed = live_planned_queue_empty(plan)
   288→    if boundary_crossed:
   289→        result = reconcile_plan(
   290→            plan,
   291→            runtime.state,
   292→            target_strict=target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config),
   293→        )
   294→        _display_reconcile_results(
   295→            result,
   296→            plan,
   297→            mid_cycle=_is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, runtime.state) or force_rescan,
   298→        )
   299→        if result.lifecycle_phase_changed:
   300→            emit_transition_message(result.lifecycle_phase)
   301→        dirty = result.dirty or dirty
   302→
   303→    if not force_rescan:
   304→        if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   305→            dirty = True
   306→        if _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   307→            dirty = True
   308→
   309→    if dirty:
   310→        try:
   311→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   312→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   313→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   314→
   315→
   316→__all__ = [
   317→    "_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty",
   318→    "_display_reconcile_results",
   319→    "_has_objective_cycle",
   320→    "_is_mid_cycle_scan",
   321→    "_mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready",
   322→    "_reset_cycle_for_force_rescan",
   323→    "_seed_plan_start_scores",
   324→    "_sync_plan_start_scores_and_log",
   325→    "_sync_post_scan_without_policy",
   326→    "_sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log",
   327→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   328→    "reconcile_plan_post_scan",
   329→]
```

```
   126→def _resolve_missing_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> set[str]:
   127→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   128→    if meta.get("last_completed_at"):
   129→        return set()
   130→    confirmed_stages = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   131→    required_stages = {"observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "commit"}
   132→    return required_stages - confirmed_stages

   138→    inject_triage_stages(plan)
   139→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})

   187→def _handle_missing_triage_stages(

   233→def _scan_gate_status(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict) -> tuple[bool, int, int] | None:
   234→    scan_count_at_start = plan.get("scan_count_at_plan_start")
   235→    if scan_count_at_start is None:
   236→        return None
   237→    resolved_state_path = state_path(args)
   238→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(resolved_state_path)
   239→    current_scan_count = int(state_data.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   240→    scan_ran = current_scan_count > scan_count_at_start
   241→    return scan_ran, int(scan_count_at_start), current_scan_count
   242→

   288→def _handle_scan_gate(
   289→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   290→    plan: dict,
   291→    *,
   292→    gated_ids: list[str],
   293→    note: str | None,
   294→    force: bool,
   295→) -> WorkflowResolveOutcome | None:
   296→    status = _scan_gate_status(args, plan)
   297→    if status is None:
   298→        return None
   299→    scan_ran, scan_count_at_start, current_scan_count = status
   300→    scan_skipped = plan.get("scan_gate_skipped", False)
   301→    if scan_ran or scan_skipped or force:

   303→
   304→    _print_scan_gate_block(

   309→    append_log_entry(
   310→        plan,

   312→        issue_ids=gated_ids,
   313→        actor="user",
   314→        note=note,
   315→        detail={
   316→            "scan_count_at_start": scan_count_at_start,
   317→            "current_scan_count": current_scan_count,
   318→        },
   319→    )
   320→    save_plan(plan)
   321→    return WorkflowResolveOutcome(status="blocked", remaining_patterns=[])

   325→    plan: dict,
   326→    *,
   327→    synthetic_ids: list[str],
   328→    note: str | None,
   329→) -> None:
   330→    purge_ids(plan, synthetic_ids)
   331→    step_messages = auto_complete_steps(plan)
   332→    for message in step_messages:
   333→        print(colorize(message, "green"))
   334→    append_log_entry(plan, "done", issue_ids=synthetic_ids, actor="user", note=note)
   335→    save_plan(plan)
   336→    for synthetic_id in synthetic_ids:
   337→        print(colorize(f"  Resolved: {synthetic_id}", "green"))
   338→
   339→
   340→def _reconcile_if_queue_drained(
   341→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   342→    plan: dict,
   343→    *,
   344→    synthetic_ids: list[str],
   345→) -> None:
   346→    """Advance postflight when resolving a workflow item drains the live queue."""
   347→    if not live_planned_queue_empty(plan):
   348→        return
   349→    resolved_state_path = state_path(args)
   350→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(resolved_state_path)
   351→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in synthetic_ids and has_open_review_issues(state_data):
   352→        ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state_data)
   353→        inject_triage_stages(plan)
   354→        changed = set_lifecycle_phase(plan, LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT)
   355→        save_plan(plan)
   356→        if changed:
   357→            emit_transition_message(LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT)
   358→        return
   359→    result = reconcile_plan(
   360→        plan,
   361→        state_data,
   362→        target_strict=target_strict_score_from_config(state_data.get("config")),
   363→    )
   364→    save_plan(plan)
   365→    if result.lifecycle_phase_changed:
   366→        emit_transition_message(result.lifecycle_phase)
   367→
   368→
   369→def resolve_workflow_patterns(
   370→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   371→    *,
   372→    synthetic_ids: list[str],
   373→    real_patterns: list[str],
   374→    note: str | None,
   375→) -> WorkflowResolveOutcome:
   376→    """Resolve synthetic workflow IDs and return whether normal resolution should continue."""
   377→    if not synthetic_ids:
   378→        return WorkflowResolveOutcome(status="fall_through", remaining_patterns=real_patterns)
   379→
   380→    plan = load_plan()
   381→    force = getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)
   382→
   383→    blocked_map = blocked_triage_stages(plan)
   384→    for synthetic_id in synthetic_ids:
   385→        if synthetic_id not in blocked_map:
   386→            continue
   387→        blocked_text = ", ".join(dep.replace("triage::", "") for dep in blocked_map[synthetic_id])
   388→        print(
   389→            colorize(
   390→                f"  Cannot resolve {synthetic_id} — blocked by: {blocked_text}",
   391→                "red",
   392→            )
   393→        )
   394→        print(
   395→            colorize(
   396→                "  Complete those stages first, or use --force-resolve to override.",
   397→                "dim",
   398→            )
   399→        )
   400→        if not force:
   401→            return WorkflowResolveOutcome(status="blocked", remaining_patterns=[])
   402→
   403→    gated_ids = [synthetic_id for synthetic_id in synthetic_ids if synthetic_id in WORKFLOW_GATE_IDS]
   404→    if gated_ids:
   405→        blocked = _handle_missing_triage_stages(
   406→            plan,
   407→            gated_ids=gated_ids,
   408→            note=note,
   409→            force=force,
   410→        )
   411→        if blocked is not None:
   412→            return blocked
   413→        blocked = _handle_scan_gate(
   414→            args,
   415→            plan,
   416→            gated_ids=gated_ids,
   417→            note=note,
   418→            force=force,
   419→        )
   420→        if blocked is not None:
   421→            return blocked
   422→
   423→    _finalize_workflow_resolution(
   424→        plan,
   425→        synthetic_ids=synthetic_ids,
   426→        note=note,
   427→    )
   428→    _reconcile_if_queue_drained(args, plan, synthetic_ids=synthetic_ids)
   429→
   430→    if not real_patterns:
   431→        return WorkflowResolveOutcome(status="handled", remaining_patterns=[])
   432→    return WorkflowResolveOutcome(status="fall_through", remaining_patterns=real_patterns)
   433→
   434→
   435→__all__ = ["WorkflowResolveOutcome", "resolve_workflow_patterns"]
   436→
```

```
   288→
   289→def _prune_covered_subjective_ids_from_plan(
   290→    plan: dict,
   291→    *,
   292→    covered_ids: tuple[str, ...],
   293→) -> list[str]:
   294→    """Prune subjective queue placeholders that were just covered by review import."""
   295→    covered = {
   296→        issue_id
   297→        for issue_id in covered_ids
   298→        if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id.startswith("subjective::")
   299→    }
   300→    if not covered:
   301→        return []
   302→
   303→    order = plan.get("queue_order")
   304→    if not isinstance(order, list):
   305→        return []
   306→
   307→    pruned = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id in covered]
   308→    if not pruned:
   309→        return []
   310→
   311→    pruned_set = set(pruned)
   312→    order[:] = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in pruned_set]
   313→
   314→    overrides = plan.get("overrides")
   315→    if isinstance(overrides, dict):
   316→        for issue_id in pruned_set:
   317→            overrides.pop(issue_id, None)
   318→
   319→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   320→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   321→            continue
   322→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids")
   323→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   324→            continue
   325→        cluster["issue_ids"] = [
   326→            issue_id for issue_id in issue_ids if issue_id not in pruned_set
   327→        ]
   328→
   329→    return pruned
   330→
   331→

   336→    import_scores_result,
   337→    pipeline_result: ReconcileResult,
   338→    *,
   339→    covered_ids: tuple[str, ...],
```

```
   110→    for fid in reopened:

   114→        if fid not in order:

   116→            order.add(fid)

   137→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   138→    """Set or clear the active cluster focus."""
   139→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)
   140→    cluster_name: str | None = getattr(args, "cluster_name", None)
   141→
   142→    plan = load_plan()
   143→    if clear_flag:
   144→        prev = plan.get("active_cluster")
   145→        clear_focus(plan)
   146→        append_log_entry(plan, "focus", actor="user", detail={"action": "clear", "previous": prev})
   147→        save_plan(plan)
   148→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))
   149→        return
   150→
```

## Comprehensive Analysis: Directive/Transition System in desloppify

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's the exact mechanism for how directives trigger when switching between phases:

### 1. **The Core Directive System**

**Location**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/transition_messages.py`

The system works through a single function `emit_transition_message(new_phase: str)`:
- Takes a phase name as input
- Looks up configured messages in `config.transition_messages` dict
- Prints a formatted message to the user if one is configured
- Returns `True` if a message was emitted, `False` otherwise

**Message Output Format**:
```
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
TRANSITION INSTRUCTION — entering {phase_name} phase
{directive_text}
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
[user message about the directive]
```

### 2. **Phase Definition and Lookup**

**Location**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/directives.py`

Five main phases with directives:
- `execute`: Working the fix queue (code changes, refactors, fixes)
- `postflight`: Execution done, transitioning to planning/review
- `triage`: Reading issues, deciding what's real, clustering
- `review`: Scoring subjective dimensions
- `scan`: Running detectors and analyzing codebase

**Message Lookup Hierarchy**:
1. **Exact phase match** (e.g., `review_initial` → looks for `review_initial` message)
2. **Coarse phase fallback** (e.g., `review_initial` → maps to `review` and looks for `review` message)
3. **Postflight fallback** (for non-execute/non-scan phases only, e.g., `review_initial` → looks for `postflight`)

The `COARSE_PHASE_MAP` in `refresh_lifecycle.py` maps fine-grained phases to coarse phases:
- `review_initial`, `assessment_postflight`, `review_postflight` → `review`
- `workflow_postflight` → `workflow`
- `triage_postflight` → `triage`
- `execute` → `execute` (no fallback)
- `scan` → `scan` (no fallback)

### 3. **How Phase Changes are Detected**

**Location**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/pipeline.py`

The `reconcile_plan()` function is the primary orchestrator:

**When it fires**:
- Called when the live planned queue is **empty** (`live_planned_queue_empty(plan)`)
- This is the boundary condition that triggers postflight reconciliation

**Phase Resolution Logic** (`_resolve_reconcile_phase()`):
1. Check if workflow items were injected → `LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT`
2. Check if triage items were injected/deferred → `LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT`
3. Check if subjective review items exist:
   - If unscored → `LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL`
   - If scored → `LIFECYCLE_PHASE_ASSESSMENT_POSTFLIGHT`
4. Check triage snapshot status → `LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT`
5. Fall back to persisted phase or → `LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN`

### 4. **All Phase Transition Trigger Points**

**Direct `emit_transition_message()` calls** occur in:

1. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py:300`**
   - When `reconcile_plan()` detects `lifecycle_phase_changed`
   - After scanning and queue boundary crossing

2. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/living_plan.py:130`**
   - When resolving issues drains the queue
   - Triggered by `clear_postflight_scan_completion()` or `reconcile_plan()`

3. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override/skip.py:281`**
   - When skipping/unskipping items triggers phase change via `clear_postflight_scan_completion()`
   - Or in `cmd_plan_backlog()` at line 407

4. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override/misc.py:134`**
   - When reopening issues triggers phase change

5. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override/resolve_workflow.py:357`**
   - Direct `set_lifecycle_phase()` when workflow creates plan (→ triage)
   - Or via `reconcile_plan()` result at line 366

6. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/plan_sync.py:457`**
   - After review import sync when `reconcile_plan()` detects phase change

### 5. **Lifecycle Phase Persistence**

**Location**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/refresh_lifecycle.py`

- Phases stored in `plan["refresh_state"]["lifecycle_phase"]`
- `set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)` returns `True` only if phase actually changed
- The return value of `set_lifecycle_phase()` is how callers know whether to emit the message

### 6. **The Trigger Chain for Phase Changes**

Two main patterns:

**Pattern A: Queue Boundary Crossing**
```
[Issue resolved/skipped/reopened] 
→ clear_postflight_scan_completion() returns True
→ transition_phase = LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
→ emit_transition_message(transition_phase)
```

**Pattern B: Reconciliation Boundary Crossing**
```
[Scan completes and queue empty]
→ reconcile_plan() called
→ _resolve_reconcile_phase() determines new phase
→ set_lifecycle_phase() returns True (if changed)
→ emit_transition_message(result.lifecycle_phase)
```

### 7. **Key Configuration Storage**

**Directives config** stored in: `config["transition_messages"]` dict
- Keys: phase names (execute, postflight, triage, review, scan, or fine-grained names)
- Values: directive text strings

**CLI commands** to manage directives:
```bash
desloppify directives show          # View all
desloppify directives set <phase> "<message>"
desloppify directives unset <phase>
```

### Summary for Adding a Model-Switch Trigger

To add a model-switch mechanism alongside directives:

1. **Intercept point**: In any of the 6 `emit_transition_message()` call sites above, check the phase BEFORE emitting the message
2. **Access point**: The phase is available as either:
   - `transition_phase` variable (after `clear_postflight_scan_completion()`)
   - `result.lifecycle_phase` (from `reconcile_plan()`)
   - Direct phase constant for explicit sets (like `LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT`)
3. **Model switch logic**: Could be:
   - Read from `config["model_switches"]` with same lookup hierarchy as directives
   - Execute before/after `emit_transition_message()`
   - Log the switch in plan metadata

The key insight is that **phase changes are detected deterministically** through the combination of `set_lifecycle_phase()` returning True and the phase value being available immediately before the `emit_transition_message()` call.
